### [1] [Estimating the disjoining pressure of liquid nanofilms from molecular dynamics simulations via implicit treatment of the bulk liquid phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16500)
*Wang Jianzhou,Zuo Zufeng,Lau Denvid,Wang Yuzhu,Mo Pinqiang,Ji Yukun,Yu Liyuan,Yang Yafan*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 针对纳米多孔地质材料中纳膜分离压($Π$)难以实验测定及现有模拟方法忽略本体液相的问题，提出一种通过预拟合压力-密度关系隐式考虑本体液相的热力学一致方法，可同时自洽确定膜厚与分离压。结果表明，严格处理本体液相密度对高$Π$条件下的准确预测至关重要，新方法较现有方法更吻合文献数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米多孔地质材料中，分离压($Π$)是控制有效应力与变形行为的关键因素，但实验测量仅数纳米厚的纳膜分离压极其困难，需借助分子模拟。然而，现有模拟方法通常忽略本体液相的真实状态，导致热力学不一致，亟待发展更精确的估算方法。

Method: 在前人基于膜表面张力厚度导数求$Π$的方法基础上，提出一种隐式计入本体液相的新方法。通过独立本体相模拟预拟合压力-密度关系，再自洽地确定纳膜与对应本体液相的属性，从而实现膜厚与分离压的热力学一致估计。

Result: 严格处理本体液相密度对膜厚确定和分离压预测至关重要，尤其在高$Π$条件下。与现有方法相比，新方法在水和氩气纳膜体系中与文献数据吻合更好，验证了其定量表征表面力的潜力。

Conclusion: 提出的热力学一致方法能够更准确、可靠地预测纳膜分离压与膜厚，为纳米多孔地质材料中表面力的定量表征提供了有效工具。

Abstract: In unsaturated soil mechanics of nanoporous geomaterials, the disjoining pressure ($Π$) arising from interparticle liquid nanofilms governs the effective stress and deformation behavior. However, experimentally determining $Π$ for nanofilms with thicknesses of only a few nanometers remains highly challenging, motivating the use of molecular simulations. Meanwhile, direct molecular dynamics simulations of systems with coexisting bulk liquid, bulk gas, and free-standing nanofilms are hindered by physical complexity and computational cost. Consequently, most existing simulation approaches estimate $Π$ using simplified systems that include only the bulk gas and nanofilm, thereby neglecting the actual state of the bulk liquid phase. Building upon a previous approach in which $Π$ is determined from the thickness derivative of the film surface tension, we propose a method that implicitly accounts for the bulk liquid phase and enables thermodynamically consistent estimation of both film thickness and disjoining pressure. The method self-consistently determines the properties of the nanofilm and the corresponding bulk liquid using pre-fitted pressure-density relationships obtained from separate bulk-phase simulations. Our results demonstrate that rigorous treatment of bulk liquid density is essential for accurate film thickness determination and reliable prediction of $Π$, particularly under high-$Π$ conditions. Compared with existing approaches, the proposed method exhibits improved agreement with literature data for water and argon nanofilms, highlighting its potential for quantitatively characterizing surface forces in nanofilm systems.

</details>


### [2] [Hydrogen Chemisorption and Current-Induced Spin Polarization on NbP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16994)
*Luis Martinez-Gomez,Raphael F. Ribeiro*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过DFT计算、Wannier表面谱函数、轨道投影和晶体轨道Hamilton居群分析，研究了拓扑半金属NbP(001)表面氢化学吸附中拓扑表面态的作用。发现自旋轨道耦合(SOC)对氢吸附自由能影响微弱，但Weyl费米弧在费米能级处主导杂化通道，并将自旋纹理传递给吸附物，产生可观的氢局域电流诱导自旋极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑半金属因其表面态可能参与吸附物成键和界面电荷响应，被提出可作为电催化平台。本文旨在定量检验拓扑表面态（特别是Weyl费米弧）对氢化学吸附热力学和电子结构的具体影响。

Method: 结合密度泛函理论(DFT)、Wannier基表面谱函数、轨道投影分析以及投影晶体轨道Hamilton居群分析(pCOHP)；通过SOC开/关对比，分别考察非SOC极限下节线衍生表面态和SOC情形下Weyl费米弧两种电子态对H吸附的影响。

Result: SOC对氢吸附自由能改变微弱，表明平衡吸附描述符对SOC驱动的表面态重组不敏感。主要H-表面稳定化来自费米能以下占据的局部H-Nb杂化态。费米能级处，费米弧提供主导杂化通道，贡献于态分辨成键模式，并将自旋纹理传递给吸附物，产生有限H投影电流诱导自旋极化。

Conclusion: NbP证明了拓扑表面态在吸附热力学中作用有限，但主导费米能级杂化并控制吸附物局域电流诱导自旋极化。这为设计兼具催化活性和自旋极化功能的拓扑电催化剂提供了新思路。

Abstract: Topological semimetals have been proposed as electrocatalytic platforms because their surface states may participate in adsorbate bonding and interfacial charge response. Here we examine this role for hydrogen chemisorption on NbP(001) by combining density functional theory, Wannier-based surface spectral functions, orbital projections, and projected crystal orbital Hamilton population analysis. An SOC on/off comparison allows us to examine two electronic regimes of the same surface: including nodal-line-derived surface states in the non-SOC limit and Weyl Fermi arcs in the SOC case. We find that SOC changes the hydrogen adsorption free energy only weakly, showing that the equilibrium adsorption descriptor is largely insensitive to the SOC-driven reorganization of the surface states. Momentum- and energy-resolved bonding analysis assigns the main H-surface stabilization to occupied local H-Nb hybridized states below the Fermi level. At $E_F$, the Fermi arcs provide the dominant hybridization channels that contribute to the state-resolved bonding pattern and transfer spin-textured character to the adsorbate, producing a finite H-projected current-induced spin polarization. Thus, NbP demonstrates that topological surface states can play a minor role in adsorption thermodynamics while governing Fermi-level hybridization and adsorbate-local current-induced spin polarization.

</details>

### [3] [QPILOTS: Efficient Test-Time Q-Steering for Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14801)
*Yifan Ruan,Chenyang Cao,Andreas Burger,Ali Pesaranghader,Kaveh Kamali,Jaehong Kim,Nandita Vijaykumar,Alan Aspuru-Guzik,Igor Gilitschenski,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QPILOTS方法，通过在推理时引导去噪过程而非修改原始策略，实现了对流匹配和扩散策略的时序差分强化学习优化，在50个任务上达到90%平均成功率，并成功应用于预训练的视觉-语言-动作基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配和扩散策略是表达力强的动作生成器，但使用时序差分强化学习优化它们仍然困难。现有方法要么丢弃梯度信息，要么将策略蒸馏为更简单的单步actor，要么在评论家改进时反复微调去噪策略，存在数值不稳定或效率低下问题。

Method: 提出QPILOTS方法，保持原始策略不变，在推理时引导去噪过程。每个去噪步骤中，先将噪声中间状态投影到最终干净动作的估计值，再在该估计值上计算评论家梯度，避免在不可靠的噪声中间动作上评估评论家。包含两种变体：QPILOTS-U使用快速单点近似，QPILOTS-M通过学习的辅助网络生成可微分的后验样本。

Result: 在标准离线到在线强化学习基准上，QPILOTS在50个任务中达到90%的平均成功率，取得最佳整体性能。在六个模拟操作任务中，引导大型预训练VLA基础模型的表现优于或匹配先前的推理时方法。

Conclusion: QPILOTS通过推理时引导去噪过程有效解决了扩散策略与TD RL结合的优化难题，无需修改原始策略或反复微调，且能泛化到大型预训练模型。

Abstract: Flow-matching and diffusion policies are expressive action generators, but optimizing them with temporal-difference reinforcement learning (RL) remains difficult. Effective policy extraction requires exploiting the critic's action gradient, yet directly backpropagating this signal through a multi-step denoising process can be numerically unstable. Existing methods work around this either by discarding gradient information, distilling the policy into a simpler one-step actor, or repeatedly fine-tuning the denoising policy as the critic improves. We propose QPILOTS, a method that leaves the original policy unmodified and steers the denoising process at inference time. At each denoising step, instead of evaluating the critic on the noisy intermediate action where critic predictions are unreliable, we first project that intermediate state to an estimate of the final clean action and compute the critic gradient there. We introduce two variants: QPILOTS-U uses a fast single-point approximation, while QPILOTS-M draws differentiable posterior samples via a learned auxiliary network. On a standard offline-to-online RL benchmark, QPILOTS achieves the best aggregate performance, reaching an average success rate of 90% across 50 tasks. We also apply QPILOTS to steer a large, frozen, pretrained Vision-Language Action (VLA) foundation model, outperforming or matching prior inference-time approaches across six manipulation tasks in simulation.

</details>


### [4] [Remember, Don't Re-read: Stateful ReAct Agents for Token-Efficient Autonomous Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14945)
*Faramarz Jabbarvaziri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将自研究模式从无状态设计重构为基于LangGraph的有状态ReAct智能体，通过类型化持久状态和工具调用接口，在超参数调优（15轮）和代码性能优化（40轮）两项任务中，分别实现90%和52%的token消耗降低，同时保持相当的优化质量。无状态智能体每轮迭代需O(n)成本重读全部历史，而有状态智能体在固定大小对话窗口中以O(1)成本运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无状态自研究模式每次迭代需从零重建实验上下文，导致每轮迭代O(n)的token成本及总成本O(n²)，存在严重的token浪费问题，亟需更高效的架构设计。

Method: 将自研究模式重构为使用LangGraph的状态化ReAct智能体，通过类型化持久状态跨迭代携带实验历史，并采用工具调用接口实现固定大小对话窗口中的O(1)成本操作。

Result: 超参数调优任务中token消耗减少90%（2,492 vs. 24,465），代码优化任务中减少52%（627K vs. 1,275K），且两任务优化质量相当。

Conclusion: 结构性的token缩减源于从无状态的O(n)逐轮重读历史转变为有状态的O(1)固定窗口操作，本文提供足够详细的架构描述以便实践者实现自己的有状态自研究智能体。

Abstract: The autoresearch pattern enables autonomous experimentation by having a large language model (LLM) iteratively modify code to optimize a target metric. Its stateless design, however, reconstructs experimental context from scratch at every iteration, incurring $O(n)$ token cost per iteration and $O(n^{2})$ total. This work reformulates the pattern as a stateful ReAct agent using LangGraph, where typed persistent state carries experimental history across iterations via a tool-calling interface. Two benchmarks are evaluated: hyperparameter tuning (15 iterations, small per-iteration observations) and code performance optimization (40 iterations, large per-iteration observations containing full source code and benchmark results). On hyperparameter tuning, the stateful agent consumes 90\% fewer tokens (2{,}492 vs.\ 24{,}465). On code optimization, the stateful agent consumes 52\% fewer tokens (627K vs.\ 1{,}275K) while achieving comparable optimization quality on both tasks. The token reduction is structural: the stateless agent re-reads the full history at $O(n)$ cost per iteration, while the stateful agent operates within a fixed-size conversation window at $O(1)$ cost. This paper describes the architecture in sufficient detail for practitioners to implement a stateful autoresearch agent for their own workflows.

</details>


### [5] [A Comparative Study of Graph Neural Network Layer Selection for Interaction Modelling in Driving Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14956)
*George Daoud,Mohamed El-Darieby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对19种图神经网络层在轨迹预测中的空间和时间处理能力进行了比较研究，发现ARMA、Chebyshev和拓扑感知层表现最佳，并总结出基于求和的聚合方法、多头注意力机制以及不同跳距离权重分配等实用设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统需要精确的轨迹预测来保障安全高效的规划，但当前GNN架构设计缺乏标准化，对于哪些图网络层能有效捕捉空间交互和时间动态缺乏指导。

Method: 对19种图神经网络层类型进行详细的比较研究，聚焦于空间和时间处理能力，在特定超参数设置下探索最有效的轨迹预测架构。

Result: 五种突出的层组合被识别出来，其中ARMA、Chebyshev和拓扑感知层表现一贯优于其他层；基于求和的聚合优于基于均值的方法；多头注意力机制能够实现更丰富的交互；为不同跳距离分配不同权重显著提高预测精度。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计更具可解释性和有效性的轨迹预测模型提供了实用的设计原则和指导。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems rely on precise trajectory prediction to plan safe and efficient movement. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become a promising approach for modelling spatiotemporal interactions among road agents. However, designing GNN architectures for trajectory prediction remains non-standardized, with little guidance on which graph layers effectively capture spatial interactions and temporal dynamics. This paper offers a detailed comparative study of 19 graph layer types, focusing on their spatial and temporal processing capabilities to discover the most effective architectures for trajectory prediction. Within the explored hyperparameter setting, we highlight five standout layer combinations, with ARMA, Chebyshev, and topology-aware layers consistently performing better than others. Beyond performance metrics, our findings yield practical design principles: sum-based aggregation is more effective than mean-based methods, multi-head attention mechanisms enable richer interactions, and assigning different weights to different hop distances significantly improves prediction accuracy. These findings offer useful guidance for designing more interpretable and effective trajectory prediction models.

</details>


### [6] [Zero-order Parameter-free Optimization for LMO-based Methods: Novel Approach for Efficient Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14970)
*Dmitriy Bystrov,Daniil Medyakov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdaNAGED方法，统一了无梯度训练、自适应调参和非欧几里得更新几何，用于大规模LLM微调中的零阶优化，并在OPT-1.3B模型上验证了收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化在大规模LLM微调中内存效率高，但对步长和平滑参数高度敏感，需要昂贵的任务特定调参；同时大规模微调需要考虑到参数块异质结构的几何感知更新。

Method: 提出AdaNAGED方法，将无参数自适应优化与基于线性最小化预言机（LMO）的零阶优化相结合，实现非欧几里得更新几何。

Result: 建立了收敛性保证，并在OPT-1.3B模型的大规模LLM微调任务上验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: AdaNAGED成功统一了无梯度训练、自适应调参和非欧几里得更新几何，为大规模LLM微调提供了内存高效且无需手动调参的优化方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) has become a central application of modern optimization, enabling pretrained models to adapt to diverse downstream tasks and domain-specific data. A major obstacle in large-scale fine-tuning is the memory overhead of backpropagation, which requires storing activations, gradients, and optimizer states. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a memory-efficient alternative, but its performance is highly sensitive to the stepsize and smoothing parameter, often requiring costly task-specific tuning. Parameter-free (PF) optimization addresses this issue by adapting algorithmic parameters without prior knowledge of problem-dependent constants. Moreover, large-scale fine-tuning can benefit from geometry-aware updates that account for the heterogeneous structure of parameter blocks, which can be modeled through methods that exploit linear minimization oracle (LMO). In this work, we study PF adaptation for LMO-based ZO optimization and introduce $\texttt{AdaNAGED}$, a method that unifies gradient-free training, adaptive tuning, and non-Euclidean update geometry. We establish convergence guarantees and validate the method on large-scale LLM fine-tuning task with $\texttt{OPT}-1.3\mathrm{B}$ model.

</details>


### [7] [Unlocking Latent Dimensions: Exploring Representations of Large-Scale X-ray Scattering Data using Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14999)
*Monika Choudhary,Xiaoya Chong,Runbo Jiang,Wiebke Koepp,Petrus H. Zwart,Damon English,Gregory M. Su,Eric Schaible,Chenhui Zhu,Mostafa Nassr,Noah P. Wamble,Kelvin Kam-Yun Li,Jonathan M. Chan,Jose Carlos Diaz,Cameron McKay,Lynn Katz,Benny Freeman,Guillaume Freychet,Yevgen Matviychuk,Eliot Gann,Daniel B. Allan,Benedikt Sochor,Frank Schluenzen,Stephan V. Roth,Ethan Crumlin,Dylan McReynolds,Tanny Chavez,Alexander Hexemer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于注意力机制的卷积变分自编码器（C-VAE），在150万张X射线散射图像上训练，生成可解释的低维潜在空间，支持离线数据集探索和实时在线分析，并集成到MLExchange平台的Latent Space Explorer中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学用户设施产生的X射线散射数据增长速度远超传统工作流处理能力，亟需高效的数据分析和实时处理方法。

Method: 训练了一个领域特定的注意力卷积变分自编码器（C-VAE），在150万张X射线散射图像上学习低维表征；潜在空间支持聚类、轨迹追踪和受控合成图像生成；在无重训练条件下部署于两个同步辐射设施的时分辨薄膜形成实验。

Result: 潜在空间展现出良好组织的聚类和平滑的实验演化轨迹；与DINOv3（ViT-7B）通用视觉基础模型对比，领域特定训练的模型对散射数据产生更可解释的潜在组织；模型可零样本泛化到不同设施的实验中。

Conclusion: 领域特定的C-VAE方法在X射线散射数据的离线探索和实时分析中均有效，已集成到MLExchange平台的Latent Space Explorer中，支持交互式结构探索。

Abstract: Scientific user facilities generate X-ray scattering data faster than traditional workflows can process them. We address this challenge across two settings, offline dataset exploration and live on-the-fly analysis. We train a domain-specific attention-based Convolutional Variational Autoencoder (C-VAE) on 1.5 million X-ray scattering images to learn low-dimensional representations capturing structural variation across diverse experimental conditions. The learned latent space reveals well-organized clusters and smooth trajectories reflecting experimental progression. It further supports controlled synthetic scattering image generation across diverse structural states. When deployed without retraining, the model organizes time-resolved film formation experiments at two synchrotron facilities into interpretable latent structures. Benchmarking against DINOv3 (ViT-7B), a general-purpose vision foundation model, demonstrates that domain-specific training yields more interpretable latent organization for scattering data. Both workflows are integrated within Latent Space Explorer, a component of the MLExchange platform, supporting interactive structural exploration across archived datasets and live experiments.

</details>


### [8] [High-Dimensional Random Projection for Activation Steering in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15092)
*Minh-Hieu Pham,Bach Do,Laziz Abdullaev,Tan Minh Nguyen,Khoat Than*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiDRA是一种免训练的激活引导方法，通过在高维随机投影空间中进行激活加法，能够捕获线性方法无法触及的判别结构，从而更有效地控制大语言模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于均值差分的激活引导方法仅能捕获类别激活之间的均值差异，无法恢复在叠加假设下非线性特征子空间中自然存在的判别信号，存在根本性局限。

Method: 提出HiDRA，一种免训练的方法，通过在高维随机投影空间中进行激活加法（activation addition），能够可证明地捕获比线性方法更好的判别结构，并可与现有激活引导方法无缝集成。

Result: 在多种LLM家族和基准测试中，HiDRA consistently优于基线方法，在无需显著计算开销的情况下实现了更强的行为控制。

Conclusion: 将激活引导拓展到高维随机投影空间能够突破线性方法的限制，更有效地利用非线性特征子空间中的判别信息来增强模型行为控制能力。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a key methodology for controlling the behavior of large language models (LLMs). Existing difference-in-means based methods, however, are fundamentally limited: they capture only mean differences between class activations and fail to recover discriminative signals that naturally exist in the nonlinear feature subspace under the superposition hypothesis. Motivated by that, we propose High-Dimensional Random-projection for Activation Steering (HiDRA), a training-free approach that integrates seamlessly with existing activation steering methods. By performing activation addition in the projected high-dimensional space, HiDRA can provably capture a better discriminative structure beyond the reach of linear methods. Experiments across diverse LLM families and benchmarks demonstrate that HiDRA consistently outperforms baseline counterparts, achieving stronger behavioral control without significant computational overhead.

</details>


### [9] [PolyKV: Heterogeneous Retention and Allocation for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15157)
*Chao Fei,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyKV是一个逐层KV缓存优化框架，通过为不同Transformer层分配不同的压缩策略和缓存预算，在固定总预算下显著提升了长上下文LLM推理的性能，在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen3-8B上恢复了与FullKV之间性能差距的25.7%-54.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法对所有Transformer层采用统一的压缩策略和均匀的缓存预算，忽略了不同层在预填充和解码阶段扮演不同角色的事实，导致压缩效率受限。

Method: PolyKV提出逐层KV缓存优化框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 基于层级别信号为每层选择适合的KV压缩策略（方法选择）；2) 在固定总预算下为非均匀分配缓存预算（预算分配），实现异构组合现有KV缓存方法。

Result: 在512 token平均KV预算下，PolyKV在LongBench上分别恢复了LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen3-8B与最强单一策略基线之间性能差距的54.5%和25.7%；在128-1024预算范围内，一致超越最强基线1.7%-6.4%，对应FullKV差距的40.0%-54.5%恢复。

Conclusion: PolyKV通过逐层异构压缩策略和预算分配有效提升了KV缓存压缩效果，验证了不同层需要差异化处理的观点，为长上下文LLM推理的缓存优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for reducing the memory cost of long-context large language model inference. Existing approaches, however, typically apply a single compression policy and a uniform cache budget across all transformer layers. This uniform design ignores the fact that different layers can play different roles during prefill and decoding, and may therefore require different eviction strategies and cache capacities. We present PolyKV, a layer-wise KV cache optimization framework that considers design space with method selection and budget allocation. PolyKV routes each layer to a suitable KV compression policy based on layer-level signals, while assigning non-uniform budgets under a fixed total budget. This formulation enables heterogeneous compositions of existing KV cache methods. Experiments on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen3-8B show that, under the same 512-token average KV budget, PolyKV recovers 54.5% and 25.7% of the LongBench performance gap between the strongest single-policy baseline and FullKV, respectively. Across 128-1024 budget sweep, PolyKV consistently improves over the strongest baseline by 1.7%-6.4%, corresponding to 40.0%-54.5% recovery of the FullKV gap.

</details>


### [10] [Edu-Theater: A Data-Efficient Agent Framework for Scalable Learner Behavior Simulation through Staging Roll-Call](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15225)
*Weibo Gao,Qi Liu,Linan Yue,Zheng Zhang,Yichao Du,Fangzhou Yao,Ao Yu,Zhenya Huang,Shijin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Edu-Theater的 cohort-aware（群组感知）学习者模拟范式，通过构建群组级能力先验并结合少量诊断性查询来细化个体状态，在无需密集历史交互数据的情况下实现可扩展的学习者行为模拟，并在真实数据集上验证了其高效性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模学习者-任务交互数据对智能教育系统至关重要，但收集成本高，且受隐私和学习者参与度限制。现有的学习者模拟方法大多以个体为中心，需要为每个学习者配备模拟器，从密集的交互历史中迭代推断潜在知识状态，这既耗费数据和计算资源，在冷启动场景下也表现脆弱。

Method: 提出 cohort-aware roll-call simulation paradigm（群组感知点名模拟范式），先构建群组级能力先验，再通过少量目标诊断查询细化个体状态。基于该范式设计了Edu-Theater系统，利用大语言模型（LLM）驱动的智能体，通过教师智能体（teacher agent）和回顾性点名探测（retrospective roll-call probing）在日志上进行群组感知的学习者模拟。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明，Edu-Theater 在显著减少LLM调用次数的情况下，实现了比现有方法更高的模拟准确性，且生成的合成数据能够增强自适应测试等下游应用的效果。

Conclusion: Edu-Theater通过群组感知的范式有效解决了传统个体中心模拟方法对密集数据的依赖和冷启动脆弱性问题，实现了更高效、可扩展的学习者行为模拟，展现了对智能教育系统的实用价值。

Abstract: Large-scale learner-task interaction data are crucial for intelligent educational systems but are costly to collect and constrained by privacy and learner engagement. Learner simulators play a critical role in simulating scalable learner behavior without the need for continuous involvement of real learners. However, existing methods are predominantly \textbf{individual-centric}, pairing a simulator with each learner to iteratively infer latent knowledge states from dense interaction histories, which is both data- and computation-intensive, and fragile in cold-start scenarios. We propose a \textbf{cohort-aware roll-call simulation paradigm} that first constructs cohort-level proficiency priors and refines individual learner states through a small number of targeted diagnostic queries. Based on this paradigm, we introduce \textbf{Edu-Theater}, an LLM-powered agent system that performs cohort-aware learner simulation via a teacher agent and retrospective roll-call probing over learner logs. Edu-Theater enables scalable future behavior simulation without the need for dense per-learner histories. Experiments on two real-world datasets demonstrate that Edu-Theater achieves higher simulation accuracy with significantly fewer LLM calls, producing synthetic data that enhances downstream applications such as adaptive testing.

</details>


### [11] [AI for Social Good: An Investigation of the Causal Relationship Between Environmental Regulations and Their Effects on Air Pollution in London, UK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15257)
*Yang Han,Jacqueline CK Lam,Victor OK Li,Yiu-Wai Man*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个不确定性感知的贝叶斯深度学习框架，估计2010-2020年伦敦空气污染法规对PM$_{2.5}$浓度的总效应。结果表明，法规平均使PM$_{2.5}$降低1.88 $μ$g/m$^3$（相对降低12.35%），效应在2013年后逐渐明显，2018-2019年最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染法规对城市公共健康至关重要，但由于政策实施非随机性以及污染轨迹受气象、社会经济变化、时间趋势和多重干预措施的共同影响，准确估计法规效果面临重大挑战。

Method: 构建不确定性感知的贝叶斯深度学习框架，整合伦敦内城监测站的每日PM$_{2.5}$观测数据、气象协变量、年度社会经济指标、时间指示变量以及32项政策的每日法规状态数据。采用贝叶斯LSTM捕捉环境和社会经济协变量的时间依赖关系，贝叶斯嵌入层表示时间和法规状态输入，并引入法规状态预测分支支持基于倾向得分的非随机政策实施调整。通过比较观测PM$_{2.5}$浓度与假设无法规情景下的反事实预测来估计法规效应，并通过重复贝叶斯训练和Bootstrap重采样汇总不确定性。

Result: 伦敦法规平均使PM$_{2.5}$降低1.88 $μ$g/m$^3$（相对降低12.35%），95%置信区间为1.64-2.12 $μ$g/m$^3$。2013年前效果有限，2013-2017年逐渐清晰，2018-2019年效应最强。

Conclusion: 持续且累积的监管干预措施对改善伦敦空气质量产生了可测量的效果。不确定性感知的因果AI方法可有效支持环境问责、公共卫生保护和基于证据的环境治理决策。

Abstract: Air pollution regulation is central to urban public health governance, but estimating its effects is difficult because policies are implemented non-randomly and pollution trajectories are shaped by meteorology, socioeconomic change, temporal trends, and overlapping interventions. This study develops an uncertainty-aware Bayesian deep learning framework to estimate the aggregate effect of air pollution regulations on PM$_{2.5}$ concentrations in London from 2010 to 2020. The framework integrates daily PM$_{2.5}$ observations from Inner London monitoring stations, meteorological covariates, annual socioeconomic indicators, month-of-year and day-of-week indicators, and daily regulation status data for 32 policy measures. A Bayesian LSTM captures temporal dependencies in environmental and socioeconomic covariates, Bayesian embedding layers represent temporal and regulation status inputs, and a regulation status prediction branch supports propensity score-based adjustment for non-random policy implementation. Regulatory effects are estimated by comparing observed PM$_{2.5}$ concentrations with counterfactual predictions under a hypothetical no-regulation scenario, with uncertainty summarized across repeated Bayesian training runs and bootstrap resampling. Results show that London's regulations were associated with an average PM$_{2.5}$ reduction of 1.88 $μ$g/m$^3$, a relative reduction of 12.35%, with a 95% confidence interval of 1.64-2.12 $μ$g/m$^3$. Estimated effects were limited before 2013, became clearer from 2013 to 2017, and were strongest in 2018 and 2019. The findings suggest that sustained and cumulative regulatory interventions contributed to measurable improvements in London's air quality. This study demonstrates how uncertainty-aware causal AI can support environmental accountability, public health protection, and evidence-based governance for environmental decision-making.

</details>


### [12] [Hybrid NARX-LLM for Greenland Iceberg Discharge: Prompt-Driven Residual Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15288)
*Yiquan Gao,Duohui Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个混合NARX-LLM框架，结合非线性自回归模型和大语言模型进行残差校正，并引入物理信息提示（PIP）方法将非结构化物理知识转化为结构化提示，用于格陵兰冰山排放建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格陵兰冰山排放呈现复杂的非线性动力学特征且可观测性有限，传统预测模型难以处理极端事件和非平稳趋势，需要新的框架来融合结构化时间序列建模与知识驱动的AI推理。

Method: 提出Hybrid NARX-LLM框架：NARX组件捕捉内在时间依赖关系，LLM在PIP引导下编码冰川动力学和环境驱动因素并感知关键趋势模式以校正系统预测误差；PIP方法将非结构化物理知识转化为结构化提示实现零样本上下文推理。

Result: 该框架能够有效处理传统方法难以应对的极端事件、罕见变化和非平稳趋势，通过融合结构化时间序列建模与知识驱动的基础AI，提升了整体预测精度并生成可解释的残差。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可扩展且可解释的路径，用于将数据有限的气候预测与物理信息LLM推理相结合，代码已开源。

Abstract: Greenland iceberg discharge exhibits complex nonlinear dynamics with limited observability, challenging traditional predictive models. We present a Hybrid NARX-LLM framework that combines a nonlinear autoregressive model with exogenous inputs (NARX) and a large language model (LLM) for residual correction. We further propose a Physics-Informed Prompt (PIP) method that transforms unstructured physical knowledge into structured prompts for zero-shot in-context reasoning. The primary objective is to explore the corrective potential of this framework for modeling Greenland iceberg discharge, rather than merely optimizing predictive accuracy. The NARX component captures intrinsic temporal dependencies, while the LLM, guided by PIP, encodes glacier dynamics and environmental drivers and perceives key trend patterns to correct systematic prediction errors. This integration allows the model to reason about unmodeled factors and produce interpretable residuals, enhancing overall predictive accuracy. Applied to Greenland iceberg discharge time series, our approach addresses extreme events that are difficult to predict due to rare variations and nonstationary trends, a limitation often overlooked by traditional methods. By fusing structured time-series modeling with knowledge-driven foundation AI, the framework offers a scalable and interpretable pathway to bridge data-limited climate forecasting with physics-informed LLM reasoning. The code is available.

</details>


### [13] [LatentGym: A Testbed For Cross-Task Experiential Learning With Controllable Latent Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15306)
*Daksh Mittal,Tommaso Castellani,Thomson Yen,Naimeng Ye,Fangyu Wu,Minghui Chen,Tiffany Cai,Emmanouil Koukoumidis,William Zeng,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentGym是一个可控的实验套件，用于研究智能体（特别是LLM智能体）如何在跨任务序列中利用隐藏的潜在结构进行学习，并通过分离探索与利用的指标，揭示了前沿模型跨任务适应的失败原因、后训练的效果来源以及设计选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练/评估框架无法提供共享的、可控的潜在结构，也无法衡量智能体是否或为何在跨任务序列中改进，亟需一个能分离探索与利用能力的标准化评估工具。

Method: 提出LatentGym可控套件，每个环境围绕一个控制跨任务结构的真实潜在变量构建；设计指标分离智能体的探索行为（是否收集潜在信息）与利用行为（是否使用已收集信息）。

Result: 通过三个实证研究揭示了：1）前沿模型在跨任务适应中的具体失败模式；2）后训练可提升跨任务适应，并分析了增益来源；3）任务间反馈等设计选择影响训练动态和泛化。

Conclusion: LatentGym为研究LLM智能体如何从跨任务经验中学习提供了受控基础，有助于设计在序列化、个性化和交互式场景中更可靠适应的智能体。

Abstract: We envision continually learning agentic systems that become more useful over time: as they encounter sequences of related tasks, they should infer the hidden structure shared across those tasks and use it to improve future decisions. This cross-task experiential learning capability is pivotal in domains such as personalization and interactive assistance, but existing training/evaluation frameworks do not provide shared, controllable latent structures and cannot measure whether or why agents improve. We introduce LatentGym: a controllable suite in which each environment is organized around a ground-truth latent variable governing the structure across tasks. Our construction yields metrics that separate exploration (whether the agent's actions gather information about the latent) from exploitation (whether the agent uses what it has gathered). We demonstrate our suite on empirical studies addressing three questions: how and why frontier models fail to adapt across related tasks; whether post-training on related task sequences improves general cross-task adaptation, and where those gains come from; and how design choices such as inter-task feedback shape training dynamics and generalization. Together, these results establish a controlled foundation for studying how LLM agents learn from experience across tasks, and for designing agents that adapt more reliably in sequential, personalized, and interactive settings.

</details>


### [14] [LLMs on Tabular Data with Limited Semantics: Evidence from Industrial Car Retrofit Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15314)
*Aina Vila Pons,Ioannis Tzachristas,Constantinos Antoniou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在工业改造规划中，基于284,271条注册记录和48,716次改造访问数据，对比了传统表格机器学习与三种LLM策略（嵌入、直接提示、混合堆叠），发现树集成模型表现最强，LLM在隐私约束表格数据中更适合作为补充而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业改造规划依赖结构化操作数据而非自由文本，需要对新增原型车辆进行改造需求预测、改造包分类及工期估计。现有研究缺乏对此类隐私受限工业表格数据的系统比较。

Method: 对比四种方法：(1) 强表格机器学习基线 (树集成等)；(2) LLM嵌入特征 (Amazon Titan)；(3) 直接提示分类 (Claude Sonnet 4)；(4) ML+LLM混合堆叠。任务涵盖二分类、15类多分类、回归及月度聚合基准，并使用哈希处理保护隐私。

Result: 树集成在二分类中AUC=0.982，混合堆叠在多分类中加权F1=0.626为最佳手动构建模型；直接提示在哈希处理后完全失效 (二分类AUC=0.500, 多分类F1=0.018)；滞后特征机器学习在月度基准中优于时序基础模型。

Conclusion: 在隐私约束的工业表格数据上，LLM更适合作为强表格基线的补充组件而非替代品。

Abstract: Industrial retrofit planning depends on structured operational data rather than free text: planners must estimate whether a newly registered prototype will require a retrofit, which retrofit package it will need, and how long the work will take. We study an industrial dataset linking a prototype-registration system (284,271 vehicles) with a retrofit-management system (48,716 cleaned visits), and compare strong tabular machine learning baselines with three LLM-based strategies on row-serialized inputs: embedding features (Amazon Titan), direct prompted classification (Claude Sonnet 4), and an ML+LLM stacking approach. Across binary occurrence prediction, 15-way retrofit-type classification, per-visit duration regression, and an aggregated monthly benchmark, classical tree ensembles remain the strongest standalone models. However, the LLM results reveal a consistent pattern: embeddings remain useful on tables (binary AUC = 0.982), direct prompting collapses once semantic signal is stripped by hashing (binary AUC = 0.500; multiclass weighted F1 = 0.018), and hybrid stacking yields the best manually built multiclass model (weighted F1 = 0.626). On the monthly benchmark, lag-based machine learning outperforms time-series foundation models, though Chronos-small remains competitive in zero-shot forecasting. The results suggest that on privacy-constrained industrial tables, LLMs are more effective as complementary components than as replacements for strong tabular baselines.

</details>


### [15] [Bayesian 3D Steerable CNNs: Enabling Equivariance and Uncertainty Quantification Simultaneously](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15479)
*Abhishek Keripale,Ponkrshnan Thiagarajan,Susanta Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯可导卷积神经网络（Bayesian Steerable-CNN），通过在可导核的基系数上引入后验分布，在保持SE(3)-等变性准确性的同时实现不确定性量化，并通过变分推理和Bayes-by-Backpropagation进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可导CNN虽能保证SE(3)-等变性，但其确定性本质无法提供不确定性量化，限制了在需要置信度估计的场景（如医疗、自动驾驶）中的应用。

Method: 在可导卷积核的基系数上引入后验分布，通过变分推断推导损失函数，并使用Bayes-by-Backpropagation进行优化；预测不确定性可分解为认知不确定性和偶然不确定性。

Result: 模型在分类准确率上具有竞争力，期望校准误差为0.0263；在加性高斯噪声引起的分布偏移下，比确定性模型提升高达6.17%；利用不确定性估计可额外提升约4%的准确率（覆盖84%的测试数据）；认知不确定性与预测误差之间呈统计显著的负相关。

Conclusion: 该框架将贝叶斯不确定性量化与等变CNN的归纳偏置统一起来，且学习到的后验方差具有语义意义。

Abstract: Steerable convolutional neural networks (Steerable-CNNs) guarantee SE(3)-equivariance by parameterizing kernels as linear combinations of steerable basis functions, but their deterministic nature precludes uncertainty quantification - limiting their use in settings where confidence estimates are essential. We propose a Bayesian Steerable-CNN that places posterior distributions over the basis coefficients, yielding stochastic kernels while preserving equivariance exactly. The loss function of the model is obtained via variational inference and minimized by Bayes-by-Backpropagation. The framework admits a decomposition of predictive uncertainty into epistemic and aleatoric components. Empirically, the model attains competitive classification accuracy alongside an expected calibration error of 0.0263 and outperforms its deterministic counterpart by up to 6.17% under distributional shift induced by additive Gaussian noise. Furthermore, we leverage the model's uncertainty estimates to enhance its performance significantly, achieving a notable gain - approximately 4% higher accuracy across 84% of the test dataset. A statistically significant negative correlation between epistemic uncertainty and prediction error confirms that the learned posterior variance is semantically meaningful. The framework unifies Bayesian uncertainty quantification with the inductive bias of equivariant CNNs.

</details>


### [16] [Localizing Credit at the Divergence: Path-Conditioned Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15576)
*Yu Li,Shu Hong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hindsight Self-Distillation (HSD)，通过将教师模型条件设置为当前训练组中成功的同龄轨迹（而非仅最终答案），在长推理链中提供密集的逐令牌信用分配信号，显著优于GRPO变体和同策略蒸馏基线，尤其在简洁答案任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习为每个轨迹只分配单一标量奖励，导致长推理链中的逐令牌信用分配不明确。同策略自蒸馏使用教师模型产生密集信号，但常见选择（真实答案）仅在端点提供线索，在需要路径级指导的中间位置教师信号缺失。

Method: HSD将教师模型条件设为当前训练批次中一个成功的同龄轨迹（成功条件策略的精确样本），无需额外采样。通过提供完整的成功延续而非仅最终答案，信用信号集中在失败轨迹与成功同龄轨迹的差异位置。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-32B上，针对数学和代码基准测试，HSD在所有GRPO变体和同策略蒸馏基线中取得最佳结果，在简洁答案任务（如AIME）上增益最大。

Conclusion: 利用成功同龄轨迹作为教师条件，能够有效解决长推理链中的稀疏奖励问题，在简洁答案任务上尤其有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards assigns a single scalar to each rollout, leaving token-level credit assignment underspecified in long reasoning traces. On-policy self-distillation addresses this by letting the same model act as a teacher conditioned on privileged information, producing a dense per-token signal. But the common choice of a ground-truth answer is only an endpoint cue: on terse-answer tasks, the teacher falls silent at the intermediate positions where path-level guidance matters most. We propose Hindsight Self-Distillation (HSD), which conditions the teacher on a successful peer rollout drawn from the current training group. Such a peer is an exact sample from the success-conditioned policy, requiring no additional sampled rollouts. By providing a full successful continuation rather than only the final answer, the resulting credit signal concentrates at the divergence position between a failed rollout and a successful peer. Across Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B on math and code benchmarks, HSD obtains the best result against GRPO variants and on-policy distillation baselines, with the largest gains on terse-answer tasks such as AIME.

</details>


### [17] [Formalizing and Mitigating Structural Distortion in LLM Attention for Zero-Shot Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15633)
*Donald Loveland,Puja Trivedi,Ari Weinstein,Edward W Huang,Danai Koutra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了将图线性化为序列时，旋转位置编码会导致相邻节点注意力衰减，并提出轻量级推理时修改方法GaLA，通过偏向图相邻节点注意力来纠正这一瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM应用于文本属性图时，需要将图结构线性化为序列，这引入图带宽问题导致的失真。尽管已知失真会降低性能，但其根本机制尚不明确，常被归因于提示设计或模型规模。

Method: 分析了旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）如何将图线性化转化为与带宽相关的注意力衰减，使得序列中相隔较远的图相邻节点注意力被抑制。基于此提出GaLA（Graph-aligned Language Attention），在推理时对LLM的注意力进行轻量级调整，偏向图相邻节点，同时保留序列归纳偏置。

Result: 在TAG基准测试中，GaLA以极小的额外开销提升了性能，证明了图线性化带来的失真是可纠正的瓶颈。

Conclusion: LLM在图推理中的性能瓶颈并非源于提示工程或模型规模，而是图线性化导致的注意力错位，GaLA提供了一种轻量级且有效的纠正方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for reasoning over Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs). However, applying LLMs to graphs requires linearizing their structure into sequences, introducing distortion rooted in the graph bandwidth problem. While this distortion has been shown to degrade performance, it is often attributed to prompt design or model scale, leaving the underlying mechanism unclear. In this work, we show \textit{how} rotary positional embeddings turn graph linearization into bandwidth-dependent attention decay, suppressing attention between graph-adjacent nodes that are forced far apart in the serialized sequence. This shifts the focus of LLM-based graph reasoning from prompt engineering and scaling toward correcting attention misalignment. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \textbf{G}raph-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{L}anguage \textbf{A}ttention (\textbf{GaLA}), a lightweight, inference-time modification for LLMs. GaLA biases attention toward graph-adjacent nodes while preserving the LLM's sequential inductive biases. Across TAG benchmarks, GaLA improves performance with negligible overhead, demonstrating that distortion is a correctable bottleneck in LLM-based graph reasoning.

</details>


### [18] [Multi-Agent Framework for Audit Risk Assessment with Explicit Uncertainty and Evidence Conflict Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15640)
*Yuhan Wang,Manqing Wang,Yixuan Lu,Zhaoyue Peng,Shengda Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UMAR框架，通过三个专业Agent（MD&A文本、财务比率、CAM）独立评估审计风险并量化不确定性，利用Dempster-Shafer证据理论融合评分并衡量Agent间冲突。在美国3,200份10-K财报数据集上，UMAR在AUROC、PR-AUC和校准误差方面均优于基线方法，并能识别与财务重述风险相关的证据冲突模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计风险评估方法通常产生点预测，无法量化不同证据源之间的一致程度，缺乏不确定性感知能力。

Method: 采用三个专业Agent（MD&A文本Agent、财务比率Agent、CAM Agent）分别生成带校准不确定性估计的独立风险评分，再通过基于Dempster-Shafer证据理论的不确定性聚合器融合评分并显式测量Agent间冲突。

Result: AUROC 0.782，PR-AUC 0.341，预期校准误差ECE=0.052，均优于逻辑回归、XGBoost、FinBERT及单/双Agent LLM基线。

Conclusion: UMAR能够提供可解释的、具有校准不确定性的审计风险信号，证据冲突模式与实际财务重述风险相关，可为审计师提供可操作的决策支持。

Abstract: Audit risk assessment increasingly benefits from combining heterogeneous evidence sources, yet existing approaches typically produce point predictions without quantifying how well different evidence streams agree. We propose UMAR (Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Agent Risk Assessment), a framework that employs three specialized agents: an MD&A Text Agent, a Financial Ratio Agent, and a CAM Agent, each producing independent risk scores with calibrated uncertainty estimates. An Uncertainty Aggregator based on Dempster-Shafer evidence theory fuses these scores while explicitly measuring inter-agent conflict. We evaluate UMAR on a U.S. dataset of 3,200 firm-year observations from SEC 10-K filings (2019-2023), with financial restatement as the target label. Experimental results show that UMAR achieves an AUROC of 0.782 and a PR-AUC of 0.341, outperforming logistic regression, XGBoost, FinBERT, and single-agent and dual-agent LLM baselines. UMAR attains the lowest expected calibration error (ECE = 0.052) among all methods and identifies evidence-conflict patterns that correlate with actual restatement risk, offering auditors potentially actionable and interpretable risk signals.

</details>


### [19] [MosaicQuant: Inlier-Outlier Disaggregation for Unified 4-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15652)
*Yangjia Hu,Haodong Wang,Zicong Hong,Qianli Liu,Quanxin Shou,Jian Lin,Song Guo,Xiaowei Shen,Xiangjun Huang,Dian Wang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MosaicQuant提出了一种统一的4-bit LLM量化范式，通过将权重矩阵分解为密集4-bit基分量（捕捉常见值）和稀疏4-bit残差分量（补偿异常值的量化误差），并设计ZipperEngine将稀疏块计算融合到密集GEMM内核中，实现了接近FP16精度且最高1.24倍加速的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4-bit量化在捕捉密集常见值（inliers）和罕见大值（outliers）时存在精度损失；混合精度方法虽能保留异常值的高精度，但破坏了低比特执行的统一性，带来精度转换和额外数据移动，削弱了实际加速效果。

Method: 提出"内点-异常点解聚"原则，将完整权重矩阵量化为密集4-bit基分量，再引入稀疏4-bit残差分量选择性补偿量化误差最大的权重块；设计ZipperEngine通过重叠流水线将稀疏块计算融合到密集4-bit GEMM内核中，实现表示与执行的统一。

Result: 在LLaMA3和Qwen3上的实验表明，MosaicQuant保持了接近FP16的精度，同时相比W16A16基线实现了最高1.24倍的加速。

Conclusion: MosaicQuant通过统一表示和执行的双重统一，克服了低比特量化中精度与效率的权衡瓶颈，为4-bit LLM量化提供了可行的新范式。

Abstract: 4-bit quantization significantly reduces the memory footprint and accelerates the inference of large language models (LLMs). However, its limited bit-width representation struggles to faithfully capture both dense common values (\emph{inliers}) and rare large-magnitude values (\emph{outliers}), causing substantial accuracy degradation. Existing mixed-precision methods mitigate this by retaining outliers in high precision, but at the cost of breaking the uniformity of low-bit execution, introducing precision conversion and extra data movement that undermine practical speedup. We propose \textbf{MosaicQuant}, a unified 4-bit LLM quantization paradigm built on a novel principle of \emph{inlier--outlier disaggregation}. Rather than elevating outlier precision, MosaicQuant quantizes the full weight matrix into a dense 4-bit base component, where inliers are captured faithfully while outlier are inevitably quantized. A sparse 4-bit residual component is then introduced to compensate for these quantization errors, selectively targeting the most error-critical weight blocks where output distortion is shown to be concentrated. However, a unified representation alone is insufficient, as naïvely executing the sparse residual as a separate kernel still breaks the unified low-bit inference pipeline. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{ZipperEngine}, which fuses sparse block computation into the dense 4-bit GEMM kernel via an overlapped pipeline, unifying not only the representation but also the execution into a single coherent low-bit inference pipeline. Extensive experiments on LLaMA3 and Qwen3 demonstrate that MosaicQuant preserves near-FP16 accuracy while achieving up to $1.24\times$ speedup over the W16A16 baseline.

</details>


### [20] [David vs. Goliath in Next Activity Prediction: Argmax vs. LSTM, Transformer, and LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15868)
*Hans Weytjens,Ingo Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了LLMs、Transformers、LSTMs和简单基线（argmax）在下一个活动预测任务中的表现，发现预训练和模型规模并未带来一致优势，简单基线在多数数据集上能与十亿参数LLMs匹敌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预测性流程监控领域已从经典机器学习发展到LLMs，但缺乏一个统一的基准来直接比较LLMs、Transformers、LSTMs和简单基线在序列建模设定下的下一个活动预测性能。

Method: 在7个真实事件日志上，系统比较了词汇适配的LLMs、从头训练的Transformers、LLM蒸馏的Transformers、LSTMs以及基于计数的argmax基线模型。

Result: 预训练未带来一致性能提升；模型规模对性能影响甚微；argmax基线在大多数数据集上匹配或接近十亿参数LLMs的性能。

Conclusion: 简单基线在NAP任务上表现惊人地好，提示研究者应审慎评估模型复杂度的实际收益，避免过度追求大规模预训练模型。

Abstract: Next activity prediction (NAP) is a cornerstone of predictive process monitoring (PPM), enabling organizations to move from retrospective analysis to proactive process steering. The PPM field has progressed from classical machine learning through deep learning architectures such as LSTMs and Transformers to large language models (LLMs). Despite growing model complexity, no benchmark jointly compares LLMs, Transformers, LSTMs, and simple baselines in a direct sequence modeling setting for NAP. In this paper, we fill this gap with a systematic benchmark. We compare vocabulary-adapted LLMs, Transformers trained from scratch, LLM-distilled Transformers, and LSTMs against a simple counting-based argmax baseline across seven real-life event logs. Our results tell a David vs. Goliath story: pretraining confers no consistent improvement over training from scratch, model size shows little effect on performance, and on most datasets the argmax baseline matches or approaches the performance of billion-parameter LLMs.

</details>


### [21] [Intelligence Is Not the Bottleneck: Validating an LLM First-Pass Manuscript Score Against Peer-Review Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15887)
*Costa Georgantas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了AIPR系统——一个仅通过提示（prompt）方式对提交稿件进行0-100分质量评分并生成加权总分的LLM系统——在机器学习顶会ICLR的300篇投稿中，其总体评分能有效区分被接收与被拒绝的论文（AUROC 0.82），评分随接收层级单调递增，且与评审平均分趋势一致。有效性主要来自基础模型本身，而工程化流程提供了更高的可靠性和结构化的评审文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助同行评审的研究大多评估生成的评论文本质量，而非系统给出的数值评分有效性。作者旨在验证一个纯提示驱动、未经过评审或决策数据微调的评分系统AIPR，其输出的数值分数是否能与真实会议决策结果一致。

Method: 使用AIPR系统对300篇ICLR投稿进行评分，输出五个0-100质量维度和一个加权总分；在评分前预先注册假设；将系统评分与官方的接收/拒绝决策层级及评审均分进行对比分析，计算AUROC、单调性等指标；同时与仅用单段提示的基线进行对比，并评估评分跨运行的可重复性。

Result: 总体评分区分接收与拒绝的AUROC为0.82（95% CI 0.78-0.87）；评分随接收层级单调递增；最低分五分之一的论文被拒绝率远高于基率，且其中无口头报告论文；评分有效性主要来自模型本身（单段提示基线与完整流水线差距不显著，p=0.09）；完整流水线的评分跨运行标准差仅0.7分，远低于基线提示的2.8分；同时生成结构化、有证据支撑的评审文本。

Conclusion: AIPR的数值评分能有效预测真实同行评审结果，其信号主要来自LLM模型能力，而工程化流程显著提升了评分可靠性和评审文本的结构化程度，可作为辅助人类决策的工具，但最终决策仍需由人掌握。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly proposed to assist peer review, yet most evaluations judge the prose of machine-generated review text, not the validity of the numeric score a system assigns. We validate AIPR, which reads a submitted manuscript and emits five 0-100 quality dimensions and a weighted overall score, against the public decision outcomes of a major machine learning venue. AIPR grades by prompting alone, with no fine-tuning on reviews or decisions. Across 300 ICLR submissions with public decision tiers and reviewer ratings, graded under a frozen pipeline with hypotheses pre-registered before any score met any outcome, the overall score separates rejected from accepted submissions (AUROC 0.82, 95% CI 0.78-0.87), rises monotonically across tiers, and tracks the mean reviewer rating. The signal is strongest where we claim it: the lowest-scoring fifth is rejected far above the base rate, with oral papers absent. The validity comes mostly from the model: a one-paragraph prompt on the same model discriminates almost as well as the full pipeline (the small gap favours the pipeline but does not meet the pre-declared criterion, p = 0.09). What the engineering adds is reliability and a grounded review: AIPR's score barely moves across repeated runs (0.7 vs. 2.8 points within-paper SD) where the bare prompt swings, and the same pass returns a rubric-structured, evidence-grounded review rather than a bare number, with the human keeping the decision.

</details>


### [22] [Scalar-pathway fidelity improves physical accuracy in short-range equivariant interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15892)
*Jia Bi,Alin Marin Elena,Samuel Pinilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理感知邻域聚合（PAN）和物理引导频谱混合（PGS）两种轻量级标量路径修正方法，在不改变等变张量主干的前提下，仅作用于ℓ=0标量通道，在MACE、Allegro、NequIP等短程等变原子间势模型上，力误差降低22-27%，能量误差降低19-22%，应力误差降低27-28%，额外计算成本仅约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变神经网络势虽改进了局部几何表征，但其最终可部署的能量曲面通过不变标量通道体现，而标量通道的聚合与频谱分辨率尚未得到充分研究，存在性能瓶颈。

Method: 以MACE为高体阶机制骨架，引入PAN（对ℓ=0通道添加配位数敏感的幅度调制）和PGS（在边和读出标量特征中增加径向和锥形频谱基），作为受控标量路径探针，保持等变张量主干不变。

Result: 在金属Ag、共价Si、短程离子LiF/Li-F子集及MD17/rMD17分子上，MACE力误差降低22-27%，能量误差降低19-22%，应力误差降低27-28%，额外推理FLOPs成本约5%。在Allegro和NequIP上取得方向一致的提升，但效果幅度依赖架构。

Conclusion: 标量路径保真度是短程等变原子间势的一个实用设计维度，该方法具有跨架构可移植性。

Abstract: Accurate interatomic potentials enable molecular dynamics of materials, molecules, and interfaces beyond density-functional-theory length and time scales. Equivariant neural network potentials have improved the representation of local geometry. However, their deployable energy surfaces ultimately manifest through invariant scalar channels, whose aggregation and spectral resolution remain comparatively underexamined. Here we use Physics-Aware Neighborhood (PAN) pooling and Physics-Guided Spectral (PGS) mixers as controlled scalar-pathway probes: lightweight, symmetry-preserving modifications that act only on \(\ell=0\) channels while leaving the equivariant tensor backbone unchanged. Using MACE as a high-body-order mechanistic scaffold, PAN adds coordination-sensitive amplitude modulation, whereas PGS augments edge and readout scalar features with radial and tapered spectral bases. Across metallic Ag, covalent Si, a short-range ionic LiF/Li--F subset, and MD17/rMD17 molecules, this scalar-pathway correction reduces MACE force errors by 22--27\% and energy errors by 19--22\%; on systems with stress labels, stress errors decrease by 27--28\%, at approximately 5\% additional inference-FLOPs cost. Directionally consistent gains in Allegro and NequIP further indicate that the correction is portable across distinct short-range equivariant backbones, although effect sizes remain architecture-dependent. These results identify scalar-pathway fidelity as a practical design dimension for short-range equivariant interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [23] [Reinforcement Learning for LLM-based Event Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15917)
*Amit Arnold Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用GRPO算法微调1.5B-14B参数规模的LLM，结合Wikipedia修订工具和新闻摘要获取实时信息，使模型能够预测训练截止日期后的真实事件。通过GRPO训练，1.5B参数的Qwen 2.5在预测性能上超越了Claude Sonnet 3.5（以市场共识概率的交叉熵衡量）。文章还探讨了预测中的尺度定律、可验证/不可验证领域分类以及固有偶然不确定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GRPO这一样本和内存高效的强化学习方法是否能使小规模LLM在超越其知识截止日期的未来事件预测任务上达到甚至超越大型模型的性能，并系统研究LLM在预测任务上的扩展能力及其不确定性分类问题。

Method: 采用GRPO算法微调1.5B至14B参数的预训练LLM；通过Wikipedia修订工具和新闻摘要接口使模型获取实时信息；使用市场共识概率作为监督信号计算交叉熵损失；在多类型预测任务（真实事件及模拟动态问题）上进行训练与评估。

Result: 1.5B参数的Qwen 2.5经GRPO微调后，在预测性能上超越了Claude Sonnet 3.5；揭示了LLM在预测任务上的尺度定律特性；对判断性预测在可验证/不可验证领域分类中的定位给出了见解。

Conclusion: GRPO能够有效提升小规模LLM的预测能力，使其超越更大规模模型；预测任务中存在固有的偶然不确定性（如掷骰子），需要在可验证/不可验证域分类中进一步细化考量；文中还报告了实现该结果过程中的多个失败尝试。

Abstract: We use Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), a recently devised sample and memory efficient reinforcement learning method, to finetune pretrained LLMs in the range of 1.5B to 14B parameters equipped with the ability to get current information through the use of a Wikipedia revisions tool, or news summaries, to forecast real events beyond the knowledge cutoff of the LLM, as well as problems made to simulate different aspects of the dynamics of that training.
  We use the results of these experiments to comment on the scaling capability of LLMs for forecasting, as well as classify how judgmental forecasting fits into the verifiable/unverifiable domain taxonomy, considering the impact of the inherent aleatoric uncertainty when forecasting future events (e.g. the roll of a die).
  As a result of the GRPO training, we manage to bring a 1.5B parameter transformer (Qwen 2.5 1.5B) to forecasting performance superior to Claude Sonnet 3.5 over the same dataset as measured by cross entropy from the market agreed probabilities. We also discuss various dead ends on the path to this result.

</details>


### [24] [The Information-Theoretic Benefit of Shared Representations under Orthogonality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16028)
*Thomas Dittrich,Oliver Potocki,Philipp Grohs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在正交性约束下，联合逼近（多任务学习）相比独立逼近需要更少的描述长度（参数复杂度），通过构造Rademacher-Haar小波与Sawtooth-Walsh读出函数的组合函数类，揭示了共享隐式硬特征时组合式多输出架构在描述长度上的效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构日益多任务和多模态，但联合近似与独立近似的参数复杂度对比尚不清晰，尤其是在需要满足等变性、守恒律或正交性等结构约束的场景下。

Method: 构建正交函数类：用Rademacher-Haar小波级数实现共享的硬特征，用Sawtooth-Walsh读出函数实现输出坐标的正交性；通过信息论框架推导联合与独立编码的最优逼近率之间的严格差距；利用Heaviside激活函数的神经网络通过三角波逼近归约实现该分离。

Result: 在正交性约束下，联合逼近所需的描述长度（比特数）严格少于独立逼近，前提是任务共享一个隐式硬特征。这表明组合式多输出架构在描述长度效率上具有优势。

Conclusion: 为组合式多输出架构的描述长度效率提供了理论依据，阐明了神经网络在几何约束下如何保持表达能力。

Abstract: Modern deep learning architectures are increasingly multi-task and multi-modal, using a pretrained foundation model combined with task-specific, fine-tuned models. Empirically, exploiting similarity across different problems, instead of solving them individually, can significantly improve overall performance. While the generalization and sample complexity properties of multitask learning have been widely studied, the parametric complexity of joint approximation in comparison to separate approximation remains less well understood. The question is particularly relevant in modern deep learning, where models are increasingly required to satisfy structural constraints such as equivariance, conservation laws, or orthogonality. We prove lower and upper bounds on the description-length for separate and joint approximation classes, respectively, in uniform norm. We build a class of orthogonal functions by composing a shared hard feature, realized by a Rademacher-Haar wavelet series, with Sawtooth-Walsh readouts to enforce orthogonality of output coordinates. The dyadic tree structure of the Rademacher-Haar wavelet concentrates the approximation hardness in the common feature component, while the readouts act as task-specific heads. Using an information-theoretic framework, we obtain a sharp gap between the optimal approximation rates achievable by joint and separate coding. Finally, we realize this separation in a neural network model using Heaviside activations via reduction to triangle-wave approximation. Our results show that even under an orthogonality constraint joint approximation requires strictly fewer bits in compositional architectures, provided the tasks share a latent hard feature. This provides theoretical insight into the description-length-efficiency of compositional multi-output architectures and clarifies how neural networks can retain expressivity under geometric constraints.

</details>


### [25] [Active Learning with Low-Rank Structure for Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16045)
*Vincent Cohen-Addad,Sasidhar Kunapuli,Vahab Mirrokni,Mahdi Nikdan,David P. Woodruff,Samson Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低秩近似和残差采样的新数据选择框架，通过行子集选择和损失保持核心集构建，在满足一定正则条件下，能够选择 $\tilde{O}(k + 1/\varepsilon^2)$ 个加权数据点，使得平均损失达到 $(1+\varepsilon)$ 相对误差，并在多个真实数据集上优于基于均匀采样或聚类敏感性采样的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于聚类（如 k-center 和 k-means）的数据选择方法依赖于数据集的几何结构假设，而许多现代数据集具有全局代数结构，更适合通过低秩近似或主成分分析来利用。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出适应代数结构的数据选择框架。

Method: 基于低秩近似和残差采样的数据选择框架，从行子集选择和损失保持核心集构建的角度出发。给定满足类似 Lipschitz 连续性的代数或角度正则条件的嵌入表示，通过加权子集选择实现近似误差保证。

Result: 选择 $\tilde{O}(k + 1/\varepsilon^2)$ 个加权数据点，其平均损失相对于全数据集的平均损失达到 $(1+\varepsilon)$ 相对误差，附加 $\varepsilon \Phi_k$ 项（$\Phi_k$ 为嵌入矩阵的最优秩-$k$ 近似代价）。在多个真实数据集上，该方法优于均匀采样和基于聚类的敏感性采样。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于低秩近似和残差采样的数据选择框架，有效处理了具有代数结构的现代数据集，提供了理论保证和实证优势，为数据选择问题提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: In the data selection problem, the objective is to choose a small, representative subset of data that can be used to efficiently train a machine learning model. Sener and Savarese [ICLR 2018] showed that, given an embedding representation of the data and suitable geometric assumptions, heuristics based on $k$-center clustering can be used to perform data selection. This perspective was further explored by Axiotis et. al. [ICML 2024], who proposed a data selection approach based on $k$-means clustering and sensitivity sampling. However, these methods rely on the assumption that the dataset exhibits intrinsic geometric structure that can be effectively captured by clustering, whereas many modern datasets instead possess global algebraic structure that is better exploited by low-rank approximation or principal component analysis.
  In this paper, we introduce a new data selection framework based on low-rank approximation and residual-based sampling, formulated through the lens of row subset selection and loss-preserving coreset construction. Given an embedding representation of the data satisfying mild regularity conditions, which can be interpreted as algebraic or angular notions of Lipschitz continuity, we show that it is possible to select a weighted subset of $\tilde{O}\left(k + \frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ data points whose average loss approximates the average loss over the full dataset within a $(1+\varepsilon)$ relative error, up to an additive $\varepsilon Φ_k$ term, where $Φ_k$ denotes the optimal rank-$k$ approximation cost of the embedding matrix. We complement these theoretical guarantees with empirical evaluations, demonstrating that on a range of real-world datasets, our data selection approach achieves improved performance over prior strategies based on uniform sampling or clustering-based sensitivity sampling.

</details>


### [26] [Mojo: A Promising Tool for Scalable Financial AI Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16059)
*Henry Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Mojo语言作为量化金融领域解决“两种语言税”（Python研究与C++生产之间的转换成本）的方案。Mojo兼容Python语法，兼具底层系统控制能力，通过MLIR编译基础设施支持标量、SIMD、多核和GPU等多种执行目标。在苹果芯片上，Mojo在核心金融AI工作负载上相比纯Python实现了20倍至180倍的加速，并提供了开源确定性还原核库mojo-deterministic。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化金融领域长期存在“两种语言税”问题：研究阶段使用Python建模，生产阶段需重写为C++，导致数值差异和效率损失。GPU加速深度学习加剧了这一问题，因为非确定性浮点归约会在长回测中产生漂移，挑战监管可复现性和审计要求。

Method: 本文调研了Mojo语言（Modular公司2026年发布的类Python系统语言）作为解决方案。Mojo兼具Python的易用性和底层系统控制能力，支持构建比特精确的确定性核。其MLIR编译基础设施使单一代码库能够针对标量、SIMD、多核和GPU执行进行编译，消除研发到生产之间的转换瓶颈。

Result: 在苹果芯片上，Mojo在蒙特卡洛期权定价、LLM情感推理、多资产回测和投资组合VaR四项核心金融AI工作负载中，相比纯Python实现了20倍至180倍的加速。同时发布了开源库mojo-deterministic，提供可复现的确定性归约核。

Conclusion: Mojo能够有效解决量化金融的“两种语言税”问题，大幅提升性能的同时支持确定性计算，满足监管可复现性要求。但文章也坦率评估了Mojo目前尚未解决的问题和局限性。

Abstract: For thirty years, quantitative finance has paid a costly two-language tax: models researched in Python are rewritten in C++ for production, often introducing numerical discrepancies. GPU-accelerated deep learning exacerbates this problem, as nondeterministic floating-point reductions can produce drift in long backtests, challenging regulatory reproducibility and auditability expectations. This article surveys Mojo, Modular's 2026 Python-like systems language, as a structural response for capital markets engineering. While closing the Python-to-C++ performance gap, Mojo uniquely combines native interoperability with the low-level systems control required to construct bit-exact deterministic kernels. Its MLIR compilation infrastructure further allows a single codebase to target scalar, SIMD, multicore, and GPU execution, reducing the translation bottleneck between research and production. We benchmark four core financial AI workloads: Monte Carlo option pricing, LLM sentiment inference, multi-asset backtesting, and portfolio Value at Risk. On Apple Silicon, Mojo demonstrates 20x to 180x speedups over pure Python on directly measured kernels; larger-scale GPU workload results are projections calibrated from published benchmarks. Alongside transparent performance data, we introduce mojo-deterministic, an open-source library of reproducible reduction kernels, and provide a candid assessment of the problems Mojo does and does not yet solve.

</details>


### [27] [LLM-Powered Virtual Population for Demand Simulation and Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16183)
*Chengpiao Huang,Kaizheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的虚拟人口模型，通过有限混合客户画像模拟需求，利用LLM评估购买概率并生成完整预测分布，支持风险感知定价，在H&M数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏历史需求数据但产品信息丰富（如文本描述和图像）的场景下，传统需求预测方法难以应用，且管理者不仅需要点预测还需要不确定性估计来进行定价决策。

Method: 将客户表示为有限混合客户画像的抽样，对每个画像、产品和候选价格，利用LLM结合结构化画像信息和非结构化产品信息评估购买概率，通过校准的混合权重聚合得到需求预测分布。

Result: 在H&M时尚数据集上，校准后的LLM模拟器在所有对比模型中取得最佳预测性能，并支持样本高效的定价决策。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种实用的方法，利用LLM作为需求模拟器，在有限历史数据但丰富产品信息的场景下，生成完整需求预测分布而非仅点预测，使管理者能够比较候选价格、量化需求不确定性，并针对平均收益或风险感知目标进行定价。

Abstract: We develop an LLM-powered virtual population model that simulates demand for pricing decisions, in settings where products are described by rich unstructured information, such as text descriptions and images, and where decision makers need not only mean-demand predictions but also uncertainty estimates for counterfactual prices. Our model represents exposed customers as draws from a finite mixture of customer personas. For each persona, product, and candidate price, an LLM elicits a persona-level purchase probability using both structured persona information and unstructured product information. These probabilities are aggregated through calibrated mixture weights to form a predictive distribution of aggregate demand. The resulting simulator can evaluate counterfactual prices under various pricing objectives, including expected revenue and risk-aware criteria such as conditional value at risk. We test the framework on an online H&M fashion dataset with product descriptions and images. The calibrated LLM-based simulator achieves the best overall predictive performance among the models considered, and supports sample-efficient pricing decisions. Our framework provides a practical way to use LLMs as demand simulators for products with limited historical demand data but rich product information. By producing a full predictive demand distribution rather than only a point forecast, it enables managers to compare candidate prices, quantify demand uncertainty, and choose prices that target either average-case revenue or risk-aware objectives.

</details>


### [28] [Prediction of Runtime Parameters of Parallel Chemistry Applications via Active and Generative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16226)
*Tanzila Tabassum,Omer Subasi,Ajay Panyala,Epiya Ebiapia,Gerald Baumgartner,Erdal Mutlu,P Sadayappan,Karol Kowalski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了两种基于机器学习的方法，结合主动学习和生成学习与梯度提升回归树模型，用于预测高可扩展并行化学计算中的运行时参数。在Coupled-Cluster with Singles and Doubles计算评估中，模型达到了低至0.023的平均绝对误差百分比和高达99.9%的决定系数；结合主动学习后，仅用20-25%的原始数据集即可达到约0.2的MAPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高度可扩展并行化学计算中运行时参数预测的难题，应对训练数据匮乏的挑战。

Method: 采用两种基于机器学习的方法，结合主动学习与生成学习，从丰富的机器学习模型中选出了梯度提升回归树模型。

Result: 在Coupled-Cluster with Singles and Doubles计算上，模型MAPE低至0.023，决定系数高达99.9%；主动学习下使用20-25%数据即可获得约0.2的MAPE。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效预测并行化学计算运行时参数，且主动学习可显著降低对大量训练数据的依赖。

Abstract: In this work, we develop two main Machine Learning based approaches to predict the runtime parameters of highly scalable parallel chemistry computations.These approaches employ active and generative learning together with the empirically determined gradient boosted regression tree models chosen among a rich suite of machine learning models. When evaluated on Coupled-Cluster with Singles and Doubles computations, our models achieve a mean absolute error percentage (MAPE) as low as 0.023 and a coefficient of determination as high as 99.9%. Furthermore, when combined with active learning to mitigate the lack of large amounts of training data, our models score a MAPE about 0.2 with 20-25% of the original dataset.

</details>


### [29] [From Tokens to Regions: CUDA-Sensitive Instruction Tuning for GPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16231)
*Wentao Chen,Jiace Zhu,Xing Zhe Chai,Zeng Qu,Qiaoling Xiao,Liucheng Duan,An Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CuSeT，一种低成本的后训练方法，通过自适应token级掩码和区域感知样本重加权，在简单SFT框架内显著提升LLM生成CUDA内核的功能正确性，性能优于标准SFT和先进变体，并以更低推理成本达到前沿水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成CUDA内核的方法要么依赖昂贵的智能体或强化学习管线，要么采用监督微调目标但未能显式建模CUDA敏感性（与执行约束紧密耦合的代码token或区域）。本文旨在以低成本方式提升LLM生成CUDA内核的正确性。

Method: 首先从token置信度模式角度分析CUDA敏感性，发现其存在于token和区域两个层面：多数敏感token被高置信度预测，少数低置信度子集构成执行关键结构的区域。据此提出CuSeT，结合自适应token级掩码（利用高置信度敏感token）和区域感知样本重加权（保护低置信度敏感区域），在简单SFT框架内实现"从token到区域"的训练。

Result: CuSeT在多个模型家族和规模上持续提升功能正确性，优于标准SFT和先进SFT变体，并以显著更低的推理成本达到与前沿CUDA内核生成模型竞争的性能。

Conclusion: CUDA敏感性分析揭示了token和区域两个层次的关键模式，CuSeT通过针对性设计以低成本实现了高效的CUDA内核生成能力提升，验证了从token置信度模式出发指导训练策略的有效性。

Abstract: High-performance CUDA kernels are essential for scalable AI systems, while Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle to generate correct kernels due to strict and implicit execution constraints. Existing LLM-based approaches either rely on costly agentic or reinforcement-learning (RL) pipelines, or adopt supervised fine-tuning (SFT) objectives that fail to explicitly model CUDA sensitivity, namely code tokens or regions tightly coupled with execution constraints. In this work, we investigate CUDA sensitivity from the perspective of token confidence patterns, showing that CUDA sensitivity appears at both token and region levels, where most CUDA-sensitive tokens are predicted with high confidence, while a smaller low-confidence subset forms regions corresponding to execution-critical structures. These findings suggest that effective CUDA kernel generation should both leverage high-confidence CUDA-sensitive tokens and preserve low-confidence CUDA-sensitive regions. Building on these insights, we propose \textbf{\underline{CU}DA-\underline{Se}nsitive Instruction \underline{T}uning (CuSeT)}, a low-cost post-training method within a simple SFT framework. CuSeT follows the principle of ``from tokens to regions'' by combining \emph{adaptive token-level masking} with \emph{region-aware sample reweighting}. Experiments show that CuSeT consistently improves functional correctness across multiple model families and scales, outperforming standard SFT and advanced SFT variants, while achieving competitive performance against frontier CUDA kernel generation models with substantially lower inference cost.

</details>


### [30] [Communication-Efficient Verifiable Attention for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16352)
*Ziqun Chen,Ming Wu,Michael Heinrich,Jason Zeng,Huiying Lan,Tianwei Zhang,Rui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriAttn，一种面向Transformer大模型的TEE-GPU注意力验证加速方案，通过将注意力计算全部卸载到GPU、TEE仅做验证，并采用两级流水线（prefill）和注意力分区（decoding）策略，相比传统TSDP方案实现2.60-5.42倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE-shielded DNN partitioning (TSDP)方法直接应用于Transformer大模型时，TEE计算开销和TEE-GPU通信开销显著，难以实现高效的可验证LLM推理。

Method: 将注意力的线性和非线性计算全部卸载到GPU，TEE仅负责验证；prefill阶段采用两级流水线重叠数据移动、TEE前后处理与GPU计算；decoding阶段当KV缓存超出GPU内存时，跨TEE与GPU分区注意力以减少重复的KV传输。

Result: 在Intel TDX平台上，VeriAttn相比TSDP在prefill阶段实现2.60-3.38倍加速（6k token提示），在decoding阶段实现3.86-5.42倍加速（10k token输出）。

Conclusion: VeriAttn通过优化注意力计算的卸载策略和流水线设计，显著提升了可验证LLM推理的效率，在保证计算完整性的同时大幅降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Computation integrity of remote large language model (LLM) serving can be questionable. For conventional deep neural networks (DNNs), the existing TEE-shielded DNN partitioning (TSDP) approach uses Trusted Execution Environment (TEE) to compute non-linear components and verify the integrity of linear components offloaded to an untrusted GPU. However, directly applying TSDP to Transformer-based LLMs incurs significant TEE computation and TEE-GPU communication overhead. This paper presents Communication-efficient TEE-GPU Attention (\textsc{VeriAttn}) for accelerating verifiable LLM inference. \textsc{VeriAttn} offloads both linear and non-linear computations of attention to the GPU, while TEE performs verification. Moreover, for prefill, \textsc{VeriAttn} uses a two-level pipeline to overlap data movement, TEE pre-/post-processing, and GPU computation. For decoding, when the key-value cache exceeds available GPU memory, \textsc{VeriAttn} partitions attention across TEE and GPU to reduce repeated key-value transfers. Evaluation on an Intel TDX platform shows that \textsc{VeriAttn} achieves 2.60-3.38$\times$ and 3.86-5.42$\times$ acceleration over TSDP for 6k-token prompts and 10k-token outputs during prefill and decoding, respectively.

</details>


### [31] [MUNI: Multimodal Unified Latent Diffusion for Coherent Any-to-Any Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16408)
*Kyeongmin Yeo,Yunhong Min,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUNI是一个端到端的多模态潜在扩散框架，通过共享随机潜变量实现任意到任意生成，在条件生成上匹配/超越最强基线，并在无条件一致性上拉开最大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生成模型主要基于LLM，受限于模态特定生成器的利用和文本配对数据的需求；最近的扩散/流方法仍依赖文本对齐嵌入、全配对训练或匹配维度的确定性映射，缺乏真正的端到端任意到任意生成能力。

Method: 1) 架构层面：扩展潜在扩散到端到端多模态任意到任意生成，联合训练模态特定编码器、表达性解码器和单一共享基于流的先验；2) 训练目标层面：提出路由训练目标，使共享潜变量同时满足跨模态生成一致性、子集潜变量的预测充分性和潜在内容最小性，实现最小充分性表征。

Result: 在PolyMNIST-Quadrant-Labels和大规模图像-文本-音频基准上，MUNI在条件生成任务上匹配或超过最强基线，在无条件一致性上取得最大优势。

Conclusion: MUNI通过端到端联合训练和路由目标的设计，有效解决了多模态任意到任意生成中共享潜变量的对齐问题，展示了超越现有方法的性能，尤其在无条件生成一致性方面。

Abstract: We introduce MUNI, an end-to-end multimodal latent diffusion framework for any-to-any generation that unifies subset-conditioned cross-modal generation and unconditional joint sampling through a shared stochastic latent. Existing multimodal generative models are largely LLM-based, which limits leveraging modality-specific generators and requires text-paired data for training. Recent diffusion- and flow-based any-to-any extensions take a different direction but still rely on text-aligned embeddings, fully-paired training, or matched-dimensionality deterministic mappings. MUNI rests on two complementary contributions, one architectural and one in the training objective. First, we extend latent diffusion to multimodal any-to-any generation end-to-end: instead of the standard two-stage recipe that precomputes a frozen latent space and then fits a prior over it, MUNI jointly trains modality-specific encoders, expressive decoders, and a single shared flow-based prior under one objective. Second, we identify that the standard aggregation rules of multimodal variational inference are insufficient once coupled with a learned prior and expressive decoders. A suitable shared latent must simultaneously satisfy coherence across generated modalities, predictive sufficiency of subset latents, and minimality of the latent content. We propose a routed training objective whose structural choices align the latent with these criteria and admit a minimal-sufficiency characterization in the realizable setting. Experiments on PolyMNIST-Quadrant-Labels and a large-scale image-text-audio benchmark show MUNI matching or exceeding the strongest baselines on conditional generation while opening its largest margins on unconditional coherence. Project page: https://muni-proj.github.io/.

</details>


### [32] [Autonomous End-to-End SOH Prediction Services for Battery Systems via Temporal-Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16434)
*Junting Wen,Dan Li,Qihao Quan,Xiwen Wang,Hang Yang,Zhaohong Meng,Zigui Jiang,Changlin Yang,Tianle Liu,Diego Muñoz-Carpintero,Jian Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TC-SOH提出了一种模块化、即插即用的服务架构，利用时间对比机制和跨窗口预测预任务实现端到端的锂离子电池健康状态估计，无需手动特征工程，在四个公开数据集上MAPE降低1.91倍、RMSE降低2.13倍，并通过多项可解释性分析提升了模型透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有锂离子电池SOH估计方法依赖劳动密集型的手动特征工程和黑箱模型，阻碍了可扩展的工业部署。

Method: 提出TC-SOH模块化即插即用服务架构，采用时间对比机制（temporal-contrastive mechanism）和跨窗口预测预任务（cross-window prediction pretext task），直接从原始运行数据提取与退化相关的表征。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上，TC-SOH相较于考虑的物理信息驱动和数据驱动基线，MAPE降低1.91倍，RMSE降低2.13倍。

Conclusion: TC-SOH通过可视化、敏感性分析、冗余分析、双向探针、未来SOH探针和时间洗牌等表征诊断方法证明，学习到的特征与专家描述符重叠且保留了额外的SOH相关信息，有序的时间上下文有助于后续SOH预测，实现了高精度且透明的端到端SOH估计。

Abstract: Accurate state of health (SOH) estimation is a critical diagnostic service for lithium-ion battery management. However, reliance on labor-intensive manual feature engineering and opaque black-box models hinders scalable industrial deployment. To address this, we introduce TC-SOH: a modular, plug-and-play service architecture for autonomous, end-to-end SOH prediction. TC-SOH employs a temporal-contrastive mechanism and a cross-window prediction pretext task to extract degradation-relevant representations directly from raw operational data. To improve transparency, we connect model efficacy with representation diagnostics: visualization, sensitivity analysis, redundancy analysis, bidirectional probing, future-SOH probing, and temporal shuffling show that learned features overlap with selected expert descriptors while retaining additional SOH-relevant variation, and that ordered temporal context improves subsequent-SOH prediction. Across four public datasets, TC-SOH outperforms the considered physics-informed and data-driven baselines, reducing MAPE by 1.91 times and RMSE by 2.13 times.

</details>


### [33] [Privacy from Symmetry: Orthogonally Equivariant Transformers for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16461)
*Alexander Yukhimchuk,Andrey Shulga,Mladen Kolar,Martin Takáč*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出正交混淆方法，客户端将嵌入乘以秘密正交矩阵后传输，并设计O(d)-等变Transformer架构ConjFormer，使服务器在旋转基下完成前向传播而无法观察到原始隐藏状态。实验表明该方法消除了余弦最近邻反演，将token恢复率从35%以上降至最多1.3%，困惑度仅增加0.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型本地部署不切实际，将敏感文本推理委托给第三方引发隐私问题。分割推理虽可缓解但隐藏表示仍可通过最近邻搜索从公开嵌入表中恢复，需要一种无需噪声注入或繁重密码学机制的实用防御方法。

Method: 客户端在传输前将嵌入乘以秘密正交矩阵；提出ConjFormer，通过标量RMSNorm归一化与所有线性权重的分块正交共轭实现精确的O(d)-等变性，使服务器在旋转基下完成完整前向传播而不观察未旋转的隐藏状态。

Result: 在PubMed微调的GPT-2和Llama 3.2 1B模型上，正交混淆消除了直接余弦最近邻反演，top-10 token恢复率从超过35%降至最多1.3%，微调后困惑度仅增加0.4%。

Conclusion: 在架构层面强制执行对称性（等变性）可为隐私保护的大模型推理提供实用防御手段，无需噪声注入或重密码学机制。

Abstract: Running large language models locally is often impractical, pushing inference on sensitive text to third-party providers. Split inference partially mitigates this by keeping tokens on the client and sending only hidden representations, but these representations can still be recovered via nearest-neighbor search against the public embedding table. We propose an orthogonal obfuscation procedure in which the client multiplies embeddings by a secret orthogonal matrix before transmission. To enable correct inference under arbitrary rotations, we introduce ConjFormer, a transformer variant that is exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant via a lightweight normalization change (scalar RMSNorm) together with blockwise orthogonal conjugation of all linear weights. As a result, the server performs the full forward pass entirely in the rotated basis and never observes unrotated hidden states. Experiments on GPT-2 and Llama 3.2 1B models fine-tuned on PubMed show that orthogonal obfuscation eliminates direct cosine nearest-neighbor inversion and reduces token recovery from over 35% top-10 to at most 1.3%, while increasing perplexity by only 0.4% after fine-tuning. These results indicate that enforcing symmetry at the architectural level can provide a practical defense for privacy-preserving LLM inference without noise injection or heavy cryptographic machinery.

</details>


### [34] [daVinci-kernel: Co-Evolving Skill Selection, Summarization, and Utilization via RL for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16497)
*Dayuan Fu,Mohan Jiang,Tongyu Wang,Dian Yang,Jiarui Hu,Liming Liu,Jinlong Hou,Pengfei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: daVinci-kernel是一个基于强化学习的GPU内核优化框架，通过技能发现与技能利用的动态演进机制，使用共享LLM骨干的三个智能体协同工作，在KernelBench上实现了显著优于先前最强RL模型Dr.Kernel-14B的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化问题中，功能正确性已可假设，但执行效率的优化仍是核心挑战；现有方法缺乏系统性的技能发现与复用机制，导致优化效率受限。

Method: 提出daVinci-kernel框架，包含三个共享同一LLM骨干的智能体：Skill Selection Agent（通过BM25和LLM重排序检索相关技术）、Policy Agent（基于所选技能生成多轮CUDA/Triton内核）、Skill Summary Agent（将成功轨迹提炼为可复用技能）。候选技能需经过执行验证确认可复现加速后才加入技能库。三智能体通过结构化SFT冷启动初始化，并使用多轮REINFORCE和每智能体优势估计进行端到端联合优化。

Result: 在KernelBench上，daVinci-kernel-14B在Fast₁阈值下，Level 1达到37.2%，Level 2达到70.6%，Level 3达到32.2%，全面超越先前最强的RL训练模型Dr.Kernel-14B。

Conclusion: 将技能发现与技能利用动态耦合的强化学习框架能够显著提升GPU内核优化效率，共享LLM骨干的多智能体架构和基于执行验证的技能筛选机制是有效方法。

Abstract: GPU kernel optimization represents a paradigm where functional correctness is assumed and execution efficiency is the objective. We present daVinci-kernel, a reinforcement learning framework that couples skill discovery with skill exploitation through a dynamically evolving skill library. daVinci-kernel jointly trains three agents sharing one LLM backbone: a Skill Selection Agent that retrieves relevant techniques via BM25 and LLM reranking, a Policy Agent that generates multi-turn CUDA/Triton kernels conditioned on selected skills, and a Skill Summary Agent that distills successful rollouts into reusable skills. Candidate skills are added only after execution-based verification confirms reproducible speedups. All three agents share a single LLM backbone, are initialized via a structured SFT cold start on diversity-filtered data, and are then jointly optimized end-to-end with multi-turn REINFORCE and per-agent advantage estimation. On KernelBench, daVinci-kernel-14B achieves 37.2%, 70.6%, and 32.2% on Level 1, Level 2, and Level 3 under the Fast$_1$ threshold, outperforming the strongest prior RL-trained model, Dr.Kernel-14B.

</details>


### [35] [Tail-Shape Estimation in LLM Evaluation Is Fragile: A Protocol for Diagnosing False Positives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16511)
*Luca Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于验证LLM评估中尾指数（tail-index）声明的协议，该协议涵盖了可接受性、拟合优度、阈值稳定性和效应量要求。在标准毒性评估设置中应用该协议后，发现了三个虚假阳性模式，并拒绝了两个评分器上的主要尾形状声明，表明尾形状估计比近期文献所显示的更为脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作推动LLM评估从基于均值的指标转向尾部感知指标（如条件风险价值、奖励模型误差的尾指数估计），但尾指数参数（区分尾重与尾质量）是否在均值和标准尾幅度统计量之外提供额外的判别信息尚不清楚。

Method: 预注册了一套协议，涵盖四个要求：可接受性（admissibility）、拟合优度（goodness-of-fit）、阈值稳定性（threshold-stability）和效应量（effect-size）。将该协议应用于标准LLM毒性评估设置，使用两种结构不同的评分器族进行实证验证。

Result: 协议捕获了三种不同的虚假阳性模式（这些模式在朴素分析下会被发表），并在两个评分器上都拒绝了主要的尾形状声明。

Conclusion: 在检测的LLM毒性评估设置中，尾形状估计比近期文献所表明的更为脆弱，建议将所提出的协议作为类似设置中尾指数声明的起点。

Abstract: Recent work motivates moving large language model (LLM) evaluation from mean-based to tail-aware metrics, including conditional value-at-risk and tail-index estimates of reward-model error. We ask whether the canonical extreme-value-theory tail-index parameter, which isolates how heavy a tail is from how large the tail mass is, adds discriminative information beyond the mean and a standard tail-magnitude statistic in LLM evaluation. We pre-register a protocol covering admissibility, goodness-of-fit, threshold-stability, and effect-size requirements for any positive tail-shape claim. The protocol is the contribution of this paper; the empirical study below is a demonstration of what its gates catch. Applied to a standard LLM toxicity-evaluation setup under two structurally different scorer families, the protocol catches three distinct modes of false positives that a naive analysis would have published, and rejects the headline tail-shape claim on both scorers. We conclude that tail-shape estimation in the LLM toxicity-evaluation setups we examined is more fragile than the recent literature suggests, and recommend the protocol as a starting point for tail-index claims in similar setups.

</details>


### [36] [Multi-Modal Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Network with Mixture of Experts for Soil Organic Carbon Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16580)
*Daniele Mos,Felipe Drummond,Anton Bossenbroek,Soufiane el Khinifri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpTGNN，一种多模态时空图神经网络，用于表层土壤有机碳（SOC）预测。该方法将土壤测量点表示为具有三种边类型（空间邻近、光谱相似性、高程）的异构图节点，采用关系图注意力学习不同关系模式，通过微调的TerraMind编码器提取多模态遥感特征，利用稀疏MoE模块融合四流特征，并耦合异方差回归与深度集成进行不确定性量化。在包含~49k样本的全球SOC数据集（含非洲~26k、欧洲~14k子集）上，非洲测试集取得R²=0.762、RMSE=3.51±0.48 g/kg、MAPE=22.9%的结果，最佳单一检查点验证R²达0.864，校准后ECE为0.031（混合）和0.026（β-NLL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOC预测方法存在两大局限：(1) 依赖手工特征与经典ML或单模态深度模型，未能充分利用丰富的光谱与时序信息；(2) 基于网格的架构忽视了野外观测点的不规则空间结构。

Method: 提出SpTGNN框架：(1) 构建异构图表征土壤测量点，包含空间邻近、光谱相似性和高程三种边类型，使用关系图注意力学习各关系模式；(2) 微调TerraMind编码器从Sentinel-2、Sentinel-1和DEM中提取节点特征，结合环境协变量及位置/时间嵌入；(3) 稀疏MoE模块通过top-k路由融合四流特征；(4) 耦合异方差回归（aleatoric）与深度集成（epistemic）进行不确定性分解，并引入Moran's I惩罚项正则化空间自相关。

Result: 在~49k全球SOC数据集（非洲~26k、欧洲~14k子集）上，5成员深度集成在非洲测试集取得R²=0.762、RMSE=3.51±0.48 g/kg、MAPE=22.9%，优于XGBoost基线；最佳单一检查点验证R²=0.864。消融实验证实异构图、MoE融合和微调主干均有显著贡献；集成UQ实现校准后ECE为0.031（混合）和0.026（β-NLL）。

Conclusion: SpTGNN是首个统一基础模型特征提取、异构图注意力与分解式不确定性量化的SOC估计框架，有效解决了传统方法在特征利用和空间结构建模上的不足。

Abstract: Top-soil organic carbon (SOC) prediction is fundamental to agricultural sustainability, land use policy and fertilization planning. Existing approaches face two limitations: they pair hand-crafted covariates with classical ML or single-modal deep models that miss rich spectral and temporal information, and grid-based architectures ignore the irregular spatial structure of field measurements. We introduce SpTGNN, a multi-modal spatio-temporal graph neural network addressing both. SpTGNN represents soil measurements as nodes in a heterogeneous graph with three edge types (spatial proximity, spectral similarity, elevation), and applies relational graph attention to learn separate patterns per relation. A fine-tuned TerraMind encoder extracts node features from Sentinel-2, Sentinel-1 and DEM signals, combined with per-sample environmental covariates and learned positional and temporal embeddings. A sparse Mixture-of-Experts module fuses the four streams via top-$k$ routing.
