Uncertainty is captured by pairing heteroscedastic regression (aleatoric) with deep ensembles (epistemic), and a Moran's $I$ penalty regularizes spatial autocorrelation. We evaluate on a global SOC corpus split into three regional instances ($\sim$49k samples globally, Africa $\sim$26k, Europe $\sim$14k). Our 5-member deep ensemble reports $R^2=0.762$, RMSE $=3.51\pm0.48$ g/kg and MAPE $=22.9\%$ on the Africa test split, improving over a tabular XGBoost baseline; the best single checkpoint reaches validation $R^2=0.864$. Ablations confirm the heterogeneous graph, MoE fusion and fine-tuned backbone each contribute substantively, and the ensemble UQ stack achieves post-calibration ECE of $0.031$ (hybrid) and $0.026$ ($β$-NLL). To our knowledge, this is the first framework to unify foundation-model feature extraction, heterogeneous graph attention and decomposed uncertainty quantification for SOC estimation.

</details>


### [37] [Factorized Neural Operators Decompose Dynamic and Persistent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16900)
*Hao Tang,Yuechen Duan,Jiongyu Zhu,Zimeng Feng,Hao Li,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子化神经算子（FaNO），将谱分解为等变动态响应和不变持久响应，分别捕捉快速瞬变动力学与持久结构，在多物理系统上实现更高精度、参数效率和跨尺度泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子依赖单一主导归纳偏置，将不同物理响应耦合到共享表示中，难以刻画异构机制共存的复杂物理系统（如快速演化动力学与持久结构共存的多尺度行为）。

Method: 提出统一格林函数框架下的因子化神经算子（FaNO），将谱表示分解为等变动态响应分支（捕捉快速瞬变动力学）和不变持久响应分支（提取一致持久结构），两支路自发特化并保持跨尺度、跨域一致性。

Result: FaNO在长程自回归 rollout、跨分辨率外推和物理机制迁移等场景下均保持一致的预测性能，显著优于现有单归纳偏置模型。

Conclusion: 可扩展物理建模应从单归纳偏置范式转向因子化算子表示，以更好反映物理系统的异构组织方式，加速机器学习在科学计算与发现中的可靠部署。

Abstract: Physical systems often exhibit heterogeneous mechanisms, where rapidly evolving dynamics coexist with persistent structures. Capturing such multiscale physical behavior remains challenging for existing neural operators, which typically rely on single dominant inductive bias and therefore couple distinct physical responses into a shared representation. We introduce the Unified Green's Function Framework across domains and propose the Factorized Neural Operators (FaNO), which decompose spectral representations into equivariant dynamic responses and invariant persistent responses, leading to better interpretability and generalization. Mechanistically, we show that the two operator branches spontaneously specialize into distinct physical roles that remain consistent across scales and domains: the equivariant branch captures rapidly varying transient dynamics, whereas the invariant branch extracts coherent persistent structures. This factorized mechanism of FaNO improves prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency and cross-scale generalization across physical systems and domains. In particular, it maintains consistent predictions under long-horizon autoregressive rollout, cross-resolution extrapolation and physical-regime shifts. These findings suggest that scalable physical modeling may benefit from moving beyond single-inductive-bias formulations toward factorized operator representations that better reflect the heterogeneous organization of physical systems, accelerating the reliable deployment of machine learning for scientific computing and discovery.

</details>


### [38] [Scalable Circuit Learning for Interpreting Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16939)
*Naiyu Yin,Dennis Wei,Tian Gao,Amit Dhurandhar,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy,Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CircuitLasso，一种基于稀疏线性回归的可扩展电路学习方法，能够使用SAE特征高效地学习LLM中的稀疏电路，在极大降低计算成本的同时保持与最先进干预方法相当的结构准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于干预的电路学习方法在SAE特征的高维空间上计算成本过高，而原始神经元的多义性又使得学习到的电路难以解释，因此需要一种既能利用SAE特征的可解释性优势、又具备可扩展性的电路学习方法。

Method: 将电路学习建模为稀疏线性回归问题，使用Lasso（L1正则化）在SAE特征空间中高效选择对模型行为有贡献的稀疏特征子集，从而恢复出可解释的电路结构。

Result: 在基准数据上，CircuitLasso恢复的电路结构准确性与最先进的干预方法相当，但计算成本仅为其一小部分；还能高效揭示SAE特征之间的关系，展示可解释的语义特征如何在模型中传播并影响预测；此外，利用学到的电路洞察，在领域泛化任务上以显著更低的成本实现了可比性能。

Conclusion: CircuitLasso成功弥合了SAE特征可解释性与电路学习可扩展性之间的鸿沟，为大规模LLM的机械可解释性提供了一种高效实用的工具。

Abstract: A prominent research direction in mechanistic interpretability is learning sparse circuits over LLM components to reveal how they jointly produce model behavior. However, raw neurons are polysemantic, making learned circuits hard to interpret. Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features alleviate this, but their high dimensionality makes existing intervention-based circuit learning methods computationally prohibitive. We propose CircuitLasso, a scalable circuit-learning approach based on sparse linear regression. CircuitLasso recovers circuits whose structural accuracy matches that of state-of-the-art intervention-based methods on the benchmark data, at a fraction of the computational cost. For interpretability, CircuitLasso efficiently uncovers relationships among SAE features, showing how human-interpretable semantic features propagate through the model and influence its predictions. Finally, we validate the utility of our learned circuits by leveraging their insights to achieve comparable performance at substantially lower cost on a domain-generalization task.

</details>


### [39] [ExpRL: Exploratory RL for LLM Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17024)
*Violet Xiang,Amrith Setlur,Chase Blagden,Nick Haber,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpRL方法，通过将参考解答作为奖励支架（reward scaffolds），让LLM在强化学习（RL）训练中利用人工编写的问答语料获得密集奖励信号，从而更好地引导模型学习推理能力。实验表明，ExpRL在数学推理任务上优于SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏方法，并能提供更好的后续RL初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励RL在提升LLM推理能力时严重依赖基础模型已有的覆盖范围。现有方法通常通过人工标注的中期训练（mid-training）来教授模型分解、验证、自我纠错等基础技能，但这种方法需要手动指定学习内容，且不清楚这些基础技能的组合是否足以解决更困难的问题。因此，本文探索一种更自动化的基于RL的中期训练方法。

Method: ExpRL方法将参考解答隐藏，仅用于构建问题特定的评分标准（grading rubrics），由LLM评判员将策略模型采样的推理链与参考解答进行比较，分配结果级或过程级的密集奖励（dense rewards），从而强化部分进展、有用的中间化简和高效的推理行为。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学推理任务上，ExpRL的RL启动效果优于SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏方法；为后续稀疏奖励RL提供了更好的初始化；在多领域混合实验中，ExpRL的效果可以扩展到纯数学环境之外。

Conclusion: 利用参考解答作为奖励支架进行RL中训是一种有效的自动化方法，能提供比模仿学习更强的推理能力引导，并为后续优化提供更好的起点。

Abstract: Sparse reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard tool for improving LLM reasoning, but its success depends critically on the coverage present in the base model. In practice, models are often primed for RL through \emph{mid-training} on curated reasoning traces that teach useful primitive skills such as decomposition, verification, or self-correction. Although effective, this strategy requires manually specifying what the model should learn, and it remains unclear whether such primitive coverage is enough for much harder problems, which require combining these skills into broader solution strategies. We study a more automated approach: \emph{RL-based mid-training} using large corpora of human-written question-answer data. Rather than treating reference solutions as targets to imitate, our method, ExpRL, uses them as \emph{reward scaffolds}: references are hidden from the policy and used only to construct problem-specific grading rubrics for judging on-policy reasoning traces. The policy samples from the original problem prompt, while an LLM judge compares the sampled reasoning trace against the reference solution and assigns outcome-level or process-level dense rewards. This lets ExpRL reinforce partial progress, useful intermediate reductions, and productive reasoning behaviors that sparse final-answer rewards often fail to upweight. On challenging math reasoning tasks, ExpRL yields stronger RL priming than SFT, sparse-reward GRPO, and self-distillation, and provides a better initialization for subsequent sparse-reward RL. Additional mixed-domain experiments further suggest that ExpRL can extend beyond the original math-only setting.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [40] [A Multi-Level Architecture for Reusable Materials Ontologies -- The OntoCrafter Ceramics Ontology (OCO) as Reference Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14814)
*Thomas Pannek,Wolfgang Grond*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 针对材料科学与工程领域本体论碎片化问题（水平、垂直和机制三个维度），提出一种多级模块化架构，通过抽象层次（L0-L3）和消费受众（材料/合规）两个独立分类轴解决水平碎片化，通过合规受众应对垂直法规压力，通过七层机制解释骨架提供机制深度。以OCO v0.94本体实例化，含5,196个类、44个模块、167,348个OWL公理等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学与工程领域本体论在三个维度上严重碎片化：（1）水平方向——现有94个本体中40多个结构不兼容，新领域常从零开始设计；（2）垂直方向——欧盟法规强制要求材料、制造、供应链和生命周期数据集成到数字产品护照中，仅解决水平碎片化的本体不完整；（3）机制层面——现有词汇表只能记录事实（如BNT-BT的d33≈580 pC/N），无法系统解释背后的机制（如Bi-6s²孤对电子立体活性等）。

Method: 提出多级模块化架构，包含两个独立分类轴：（1）抽象层次——L0桥梁层、L1材料无关实验室笔记本层、L2材料类特定层、L3范畴推理层；（2）消费受众——材料受众与合规受众。材料特定层内部采用七层机制解释骨架组织（对称性、能量/DFT、热力学/CALPHAD、动力学、微观结构、缺陷化学、键合）。该架构以OntoCrafter陶瓷本体（OCO v0.94）实现。

Result: 实现了OCO v0.94本体：5,196个类（44个模块）、167,348个OWL公理（其中40,454个逻辑公理）、1,674个属性、829个跨本体桥接映射、1,172个SHACL形状、163个已发布的能力问题。

Conclusion: 水平-受众模块化解决了水平碎片化问题，合规受众吸收了垂直法规压力，L2的七层组织提供了机制解释深度。该架构可有效应对材料本体论在水平、垂直和机制三个维度的碎片化挑战。

Abstract: The Materials Science and Engineering ontology landscape is fragmented along multiple axes simultaneously. Horizontally: a recent survey identified 94 ontologies of which over 40 are structurally incompatible; each new application domain -- ceramics, polymers, batteries, smart materials -- typically restarts ontology design from scratch. Vertically: EU regulation (CSRD, CSDDD, PPWR, CBAM, R2R, AI Act, ESPR) forces material, manufacturing, supply-chain, and lifecycle data into integrated digital product passports, leaving ontologies that only address horizontal fragmentation incomplete for any contemporary consumer. And mechanistically: a vocabulary that records that BNT-BT has $d_{33} \approx 580$ pC/N stores a fact but cannot surface why -- Bi-6s$^2$ lone-pair stereo-activity, anomalous Born effective charges, soft modes, defect chemistry -- without a systematic explanation skeleton. The level-and-audience modularity dissolves the horizontal fragmentation, the compliance audience absorbs the vertical regulation pressure, and the seven-tier organisation of Level 2 delivers the mechanistic explanation depth. Abstract: Precipitation strengthening is a cornerstone of physical metallurgy, delivering otherwise unattainable combinations of strength and ductility. The approach relies on nanoscale precipitates that impede the motion of dislocations, the primary carriers of plastic deformation. However, in situ experiments cannot yet resolve the coupled, real-time evolution of dislocation networks and nanoprecipitates, leaving these atomic-scale dynamics inaccessible to direct observation. Here, using large-scale atomistic simulations that fully capture these dynamics, we demonstrate that the classical cutting-versus-bowing dichotomy is incomplete. Instead, strengthening arises as an emergent collective phenomenon driven by concurrent, multi-dislocation interactions. These interactions simultaneously induce dislocation accumulation at interfaces, storage within precipitates, and precipitate-mediated multiplication inside the matrix. These findings establish a mechanistic framework that transcends traditional models and provides a new foundation for predicting strengthening behavior.

Radial stress analysis shows that interfacial stress from mismatch localizes within 2-3 molecular layers near the interface, triggering subsequent defect-mediated growth. These findings highlight substrate lattice matching in regulating confined solid growth and crystallization quality, establish it as a key principle for ablator inner-surface engineering in ICF cryogenic targets, and offer atomic guidance for growing high-quality single-crystal deuterium-tritium (DT) fuel layers with optimal smoothness.

Interestingly, this value is significantly greater than the previously reported value for CrI3/WSe2, which corresponds to an effective magnetic field of ~ 10 T. Besides, we also have a high Curie temperature of 500 K, and an out-of-plane magnetic anisotropy energy (MAE) of 0.31 meV, indicating that this oxide-based heterostructure can also be used for near-room temperature operation. Importantly, under the external electric field with a step of 0.1 eV/Å, the ferromagnetism is preserved and an enhancement in Curie temperature and MAE makes this oxide-based heterostructure more valuable for the next generation valleytronic devices. Therefore, these findings establish a new paradigm for realizing tunable, robust, and magnetic field-free valleytronic and spintronic devices for this oxide-based heterostructure. Moreover, this concept can be generalized to other correlated oxide-based TMD systems, providing a versatile strategy for next-generation quantum and functional materials.

Abstract: Electric polarization is a static ground-state Berry-phase property, whereas second-harmonic generation (SHG) and shift current are dynamical optical responses. Their connection is encoded in the shift vector, whose Brillouin-zone average is governed by the band-resolved Berry-phase polarization difference between the optically connected initial and final states. Here we exploit this geometric relation in quantized formal polarization (QFP) crystals, where symmetry-quantized formal-polarization branches correspond to fractional Wannier-center sectors. First-principles screening identifies noncentrosymmetric QFP materials with giant SHG responses, including $\mathrm{InNbBr}_6$ and $\mathrm{InPS}_3$. Band-resolved Berry-phase analysis shows that their dominant optical transitions connect occupied and low-lying unoccupied states whose Wannier centers lie at distinct fractional Wyckoff positions, producing a large transition-resolved Wannier-center displacement. This displacement gives rise to a large shift vector and a dominant shift-vector-related intraband contribution to the static SHG susceptibility. Our results show that symmetry-quantized formal polarization can become optically active through transitions between fractional Wannier-center sectors, providing a symmetry-guided route to giant SHG and shift-current responses.

### [56] [InvDesMobility: a reliability-gated first-principles feedback framework for closed-loop materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16133)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出InvDesMobility框架，集成多智能体自动化DFT、证据分层、生成式结构提议、采集排名和可审计发布。采用可靠性门控机制对通道级数据进行筛选，将松弛结构用于更新生成模型，保留的迁移率通道用于训练采集模型并设定验证优先级。通过多轮迭代进行大规模筛选。

Conclusion: 主要贡献并非提供一个固定的高迁移率材料列表，而是提出一种可迁移的反馈合同，使基于昂贵计算属性的闭环反演设计既实用又具备可审计性。所有源代码、保留的反馈记录和工作流均已开源发布。

Abstract: Inverse materials design starts from target functionality and searches for structures that can realize it. Its value in closed-loop discovery depends not only on prediction performance, but also on whether expensive first-principles results are independently validated, provenance-recorded, and admitted as feedback only when evidence is sufficient. This is especially important for composite properties such as carrier mobility, where a final scalar value hides intermediate quantities, fit quality, convergence history, and workflow assumptions. Here we present InvDesMobility, a reliability-gated first-principles feedback framework that integrates multi-agent automated DFT, evidence stratification, generative structure proposal, acquisition ranking, and auditable release. Using 516 2DMatPedia-derived candidates, the workflow produced 280 QC-passed materials and 573 retained carrier-direction seed channels after channel-level reliability gating. These records were split into two feedback objects: relaxed structures updated the generative model, while retained mobility channels trained the acquisition model and set validation priority. Over multiple iterations, InvDesMobility screened 2.4 x 10^6 structures, submitted 102 candidates for DFT validation, and retained 86 reliability-gated generated channels across 41 formulas. Overall, the main contribution is not a fixed list of high-mobility materials, but a transferable feedback contract that makes closed-loop inverse design both useful and auditable when learning from expensive calculated properties. Here, we complement these measurements with time-resolved elastic X-ray diffraction in the low-temperature tetragonal phase, in which antiferrodistortive fluctuations are small but a finite average rotation has set in. Abstract: The acoustic flexural (phonon) modes of low-dimensional materials should show a quadratic dispersion close to the Brillouin zone center. Any departure from this behavior in Density Functional Theory calculations is typically associated with a breaking of the rotational invariance, with few methods available to correct it. In this work, we reexamine this issue based on a reciprocal-space imposition of this condition, with corrections on the zone-center IFCs and their first and second derivatives with respect to the phonon wavevector. We propose two correction schemes for the short-range part of the interatomic force constants, one based on the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse, that we implement in the ABINIT software package, and one based on the straight modification of the on-site antisymmetric part of the first-order derivative of the dynamical matrix with respect to the wavevector. We investigate the impact of imposing rotational invariance for different system dimensionalities and pseudopotentials. Finally, we discuss rotational invariance in the context of long-range electrostatics contribution to the IFCs. We observe that the usual treatment of electrostatics is by construction not rotationally invariant. This is found especially critical when correcting the second derivatives of the IFCs.

Method: 以电催化相关氧化物IrO₂为诊断案例，检验八种领先uMLIPs。通过对比AIMD参考，分析能量/力精度、能量-体积响应、压力预测等关键因素。提出两种改进方案：压力目标微调（fine-tuning）和修正的NVT淬火/NPT平衡协议。

Result: 所有uMLIPs在传统熔融-淬火协议下均产生严重体积膨胀（密度1-4 vs 10.04 g/cm³）。两种修复方案均能恢复正确的IrO₂密度和局部结构。该膨胀问题在30种不同材料中具有普遍性，修正协议将密度MAE从2.46降至0.35 g/cm³。

Abstract: Generating experimentally relevant amorphous structures via melt-quench molecular dynamics is prohibitively expensive at the first-principles level. Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) could accelerate such simulations, but their reliability under non-equilibrium conditions remains unclear. Here, we examine eight leading uMLIPs for generating amorphous IrO2, using this electrocatalytically relevant oxide as a diagnostic case. Under the conventional melt-quench protocol, all models yield unphysically expanded structures with densities of 1-4 g/cm3, far below the ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) reference value of 10.04 g/cm3. Comparisons against ab initio references show that accurate energies and forces alone do not ensure stable NPT dynamics; correct energy-volume responses and pressure predictions are also essential. We identify two practical remedies: pressure-targeted fine-tuning and a revised NVT-quench/NPT-equilibration protocol that avoids unphysical volume expansion without additional ab initio training data. Both recover IrO2 densities and local structures consistent with AIMD. Across 30 chemically diverse materials, the volume-expansion failure proves general, and the revised protocol substantially improves density predictions, reducing the AIMD-referenced MAE from 2.46 to 0.35 g/cm3. This work establishes practical validation criteria and simulation strategies for robust uMLIP-driven amorphous structure generation.

</details>

Abstract: The bulk photovoltaic effect in non-centrosymmetric materials provides an alternative mechanism for the conversion of light into a current response compared to p-n junctions. Among its various contributions, the shift current is particularly attractive because it is governed by the geometric properties of electronic wavefunctions and can generate large photocurrents in low-dimensional materials. Here, we investigate the evolution of the shift current response in mono-, bi-, and trilayer H-MoS2, as well as in twisted moiré bilayers and trilayers. To describe large moiré supercells we develop a Slater-Koster tight-binding model parametrized from first-principles calculations. The resulting electronic structures and shift-current responses are compared with density functional theory calculations and Wannier-interpolated results to verify the accuracy of the approach. The model accurately reproduces the electronic structure near the band edges and captures the main spectral features of the shift current conductivity. We show that twisting breaks the crystal symmetry and activates additional conductivity tensor components that are forbidden in untwisted structures, leading to new tunable in-plane photocurrent components. Analysis of the shift distance reveals a direct connection between the twist-induced modification of the electronic wavefunctions and the increase of the nonlinear response. Our results establish the twist angle as an effective parameter for engineering shift current generation in multilayer transition-metal dichalcogenide base systems and demonstrate that tight-binding approaches provide a practical route for exploring nonlinear optical phenomena in large-scale moiré materials beyond the limits of conventional first-principles calculations.

</details>

Abstract: Understanding the ionic transport and scaling behaviors in nanopore arrays is essential for bridging fundamental ion physics and blue energy applications. By fabricating sub-3 nm and sub-20 nm diameter nanopore arrays (NPAs) spanning from few pores to N ~ 10000, we systematically investigate ionic conductance, ion current rectification, and osmotic energy conversion. We report the ionic conductance scaling laws and nonlinearity with nanopore number, with stronger deviations from linearity at lower salt concentrations. Experimental evidence reveals that surface-charge-governed conductance and ion current rectification progressively weaken with increasing N and even vanish as the NPA scales up to N ~ 10000, resulting in an underestimation of surface charge density. In a sub-3 nm densely packed array (separation ~ 25 nm), the conductance exhibits an anomalous power-law dependence on concentration, deviating markedly from the single nanopore characteristics, attributed to the strong pore interactions. Furthermore, osmotic power harvesting measurements reveal a substantial reduction in power density upon scaling, with decreases of up to three orders of magnitude over the same range. To elucidate the underlying mechanism, we developed rigorous 3D modeling showing that the nonlinear behavior originates from concentration polarization at pore entrances and suppressed electric field across NPAs, collectively hindering ion transport. Our work provides insight into nonlinear ion-transport scaling and reveals fundamental differences between transport phenomena in single nanopores and nanopore arrays.

</details>

*Louis Mahon,Elliot Ford,Callum Hackett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合反事实解释与对话者先验信念的解释定义，并探讨了该定义对AI可解释性的影响，特别是为何LLM输出难以产生良好解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来"什么是好的解释"是哲学辩论的话题，在AI输出背景下重新受到关注。可解释性对于AI的广泛采用至关重要，但在产生AI系统的好解释之前，必须首先理解什么是好的解释。

Method: 受反事实解释概念的启发，提出一种新的解释定义，同时强调必须考虑对话者对解释中可能提供的每个事实的先验信念。

Conclusion: 好的解释需要同时考虑反事实信息和接收者的先验信念，这为理解AI解释的挑战提供了新视角。

Abstract: How to define a good explanation is a long-standing philosophical debate which has found recent renewed interest in the context of AI outputs. Explainability is crucial for AI adoption in many contexts, but in order to produce good explanations of AI systems, we must first have an understanding of what good explanations are. In this paper we propose a definition inspired by the notion of counterfactual explanations, however we argue that one must also take into account the interlocutor's prior beliefs in each fact that could be offered in an explanation. We explore the ramifications of this definition for AI explainability and, in particular, why LLM outputs are difficult to produce good explanations for.

</details>


### [63] [When Agent Automation Becomes Profitable: Quantifying and Insuring Autonomous AI Risk through Trace-Economic Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16465)
*Binyan Xu,Xilin Dai,Fan Yang,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过追踪-经济核保框架，将AI代理部署风险量化并转化为可保险事件，从而实现经济上可接受的自动化部署。该方法使用确定性经济标签替代LLM法官，在测试中将定价MAE从$17.7K降至$569，风险控制使CVaR95降低72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理在实际系统中执行不可逆操作，但代理造成的损失缺乏明确的归属、定价和转移机制。服务商常否认间接损失，用户得不到补偿，而默认的人工审核又限制了自动化效率。因此需要一种让自主AI部署在经济上可被接受的方法。

Method: 提出追踪-经济核保框架：将风险在客户-任务-追踪片段层面量化，通过保险转移风险。核心是将工具使用追踪映射为客户暴露和可索赔损失，使用确定性经济标签（而非LLM法官）进行定价、控制和风险转移。并给出有限样本范围条件的定理1。

Result: 追踪-经济定价将定价MAE从$17.7K降至$569，消除了逆向交叉补贴；300条追踪的专家审核接受295个标签不变；在1000条真实SWE-smith追踪上，追踪条件化控制将CVaR95降低72%。

Conclusion: 通过追踪-经济核保，自主AI部署在经济上可以变得可接受，前提是其预期收益超过保费、控制成本和剩余风险。该方法需要定义明确的角色、有边界的权限和可比较的追踪记录。

Abstract: AI agents can now take irreversible actions in operational systems, but agent-caused losses are still not clearly assigned, priced, or transferred. Providers often disclaim consequential damages, users are left with uncompensated losses, and default human review limits the efficiency gains of automation. We ask when autonomous AI deployment can become economically acceptable despite failure risk. Our answer is to quantify risk at the customer-task-trace episode level and transfer it through insurance. Automation is acceptable when its expected benefit exceeds the premium, control cost, and remaining risk. This requires a defined role with bounded permissions and comparable traces. We introduce trace-economic underwriting, which maps tool-use traces to customer exposure and claimable loss, then uses this representation for pricing, control, and risk transfer. It uses deterministic economic labels rather than an LLM judge. In our trace-to-loss testbed, trace-economic pricing reduces pricing MAE from $17.7K to $569 and removes regressive cross-subsidy. A 300-trace expert audit accepts 295 labels unchanged. On 1,000 real SWE-smith traces, trace-conditioned controls reduce CVaR95 by 72%. Theorem~1 gives a finite-sample scope condition. We release code, labels, and audit sheets.

</details>


### [64] [PrologMCP: A Standardized Prolog Tool Interface for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14935)
*Agnieszka Mensfelt,Adarsh Prabhakaran,Adrian Haret,Vince Trencsenyi,Kostas Stathis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PrologMCP，一个通过模型上下文协议（MCP）将Prolog暴露为有状态工具的通用开源服务器，使LLM能够将演绎推理任务符号化地委托给Prolog求解器。实验表明，在PARARULE-Plus数据集上，结合PrologMCP的formalizer agent在推理准确率上匹配甚至超越强推理LLM（如o4-mini），且在挑战性子集上表现更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿推理调优的语言模型在深层演绎推理任务上仍然失败，且通过扩展内部推理来提升性能的成本高昂。符号化委托（symbolic delegation）提供了一种互补路径——LLM翻译问题，求解器执行推理，但当前的自动形式化流程通常是针对特定任务或代理的定制集成，缺乏通用性和可复用性。

Method: 设计并实现PrologMCP，一个任务无关的开源服务器，通过MCP协议暴露Prolog推理引擎作为有状态工具。采用紧凑的工具接口、结构化错误报告和按会话隔离机制，构建"翻译-运行-检查-修复"（translate-run-inspect-repair）循环作为通用原语。在PARARULE-Plus数据集上评估，使用general样本和针对自然语言推理特定失败模式的challenging子集。

Result: 在general样本上，formalizer agent准确率1.00，匹配推理LLM（o4-mini 1.00/Claude Sonnet 4.6 0.998），远超标准模型（GPT-4.1 0.762）；在challenging子集上，formalizer保持近乎完美（1.00/0.99），而推理LLM降至0.95/0.94。

Conclusion: 通过MCP将推理委托给Prolog是一种鲁棒且可检查的替代方案，能有效弥补纯LLM在深层演绎推理上的不足，且具有任务无关性和可复用性。

Abstract: Frontier reasoning-tuned language models still fail on deductive tasks at depth, and the cost of improved performance through extended internal reasoning scales poorly. Symbolic delegation offers a complementary route: a language model translates the problem, while a solver performs the inference. However, current autoformalization pipelines for logic programming are typically bespoke integrations tied to particular tasks or agents. We introduce PrologMCP, a task-agnostic, open-source server that exposes Prolog as a stateful tool through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Its compact tool interface, structured error reporting, and per-session isolation make the translate-run-inspect-repair loop a reusable primitive for MCP-capable agents. We evaluate a formalizer agent enhanced with PrologMCP against standard and reasoning LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-4.1, and o4-mini) on two subsets of PARARULE-Plus: a general-purpose sample and a more challenging one targeting a specific failure mode of natural-language reasoning. On the general sample, the formalizer matches or exceeds reasoning LLMs (accuracy 1.00 vs.\ 1.00 / 0.998), with the largest gains over standard models (0.762 for GPT-4.1). On the challenging subset, the formalizer remains near-perfect (1.00 / 0.99) while reasoning LLMs drop to 0.95 / 0.94. These results suggest that delegating inference to Prolog via MCP is a robust and inspectable alternative to extended natural-language reasoning.

</details>


### [65] [Metric Match: A Subset Selection Approach to Evaluating LLM Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15029)
*Alyssa Unell,Natalie Dullerud,Naomi Boneh,Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsu Hashimoto,Nigam Shah,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Metric Match方法，通过有限标注估计LLM评判者与人类评分者之间的一致性可靠性指标，在四个相关性指标和15个数据集上达到0.838胜率，平均估计误差降低18.7%，标注需求减少32.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判者替代昂贵的人工评估，但其可靠性依赖于与人类评分者的一致性，而这种一致性本身需要大量人工标注。现有方法难以在有限标注下准确估计LLM评判者的可靠性。

Method: 提出Metric Match方法，从样本中选择一个子集进行人工标注，使该子集在合成标签上与总体可靠性指标相匹配，从而用有限标注估计LLM评判者的相关性可靠性指标。

Result: 在四个相关性指标和15个数据集上，Metric Match对随机子集选择的胜率为0.838，平均估计误差降低18.7%，标注需求减少32.5%；在医学案例研究中比随机选择节省$1,041.67专家标注费用。

Conclusion: Metric Match能有效从有限标注中估计LLM评判者的可靠性，减少人工标注成本，且可扩展至可靠性分类任务，优于随机选择。

Abstract: LLM judges are used to reduce the need for costly human labor in evaluating open-ended text generation. However, the reliability of these judges depends critically on their alignment with human raters -- a property that itself depends on costly human annotations. In this work, we develop a method (Metric Match) for estimating correlation-based reliability metrics of LLM judges from limited annotations. Metric Match selects a subset of samples for human annotation such that the subset matches the population reliability metric with respect to acquired synthetic labels. We empirically show that Metric Match achieves a win-rate of 0.838 against random subset selection across four different correlation metrics and 15 datasets, with an 18.7% decrease in average estimation error and reduces annotation needs by 32.5%. We provide a cost model and highlight a medical case study where our method saves $1,041.67 compared to random selection for expert annotation. Further, we shift our task from reliability estimation to reliability classification of whether a given judge is above a deployment threshold, outperforming random selection with Metric Match. All project code is publicly available, and we additionally provide an installable package for ease of use.

</details>


### [66] [Fusion is not one-size-fits-all: Cross-Modal Representation Alignment for Time-to-Event Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15038)
*Zhemin Zhang,Weijie Chen,David Le,Amara Tariq,Alex Wallace,Matthew Stib,Juan Maria Farina,Chadi Ayoub,Reza Arsanjani,Imon Banerjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于基础模型的多模态融合框架，用于CT影像与纵向EHR数据之间的跨模态表征对齐，在肺栓塞死亡率和心血管疾病预测任务上，对比单模态基线一致性指数提升1.5-5.4%，并系统分析了模态不平衡下的融合行为，强调任务感知的多模态对齐是稳健泛化的必要设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床多模态数据的时间-事件预测面临模态不平衡和分布偏移的挑战，现有方法缺乏跨任务、跨机构通用的对齐框架。

Method: 分别使用领域特定的基础模型独立编码CT和EHR模态，通过四种融合策略（后融合、对比对齐、交叉注意力、共注意力）在共享隐空间中对齐表征，并在两个大规模多机构队列上评估。

Result: 融合方法在模态贡献均衡时一致性指数提升1.5-5.4%；对比多模态融合（特别是CLMBR表征）在PE死亡率预测中表现最稳定；MACE任务中交叉注意力（one-hot）内部性能最优，图像引导的共注意力外部性能最佳。

Conclusion: 建立了任务感知的多模态对齐作为稳健泛化和可扩展临床部署的必要设计原则，首次系统分析了TTE预测中模态不平衡下的融合行为。

Abstract: Accurate time-to-event (TTE) prediction from multimodal clinical data remains challenging due to modality imbalance and distribution shift. We introduce a foundation model-driven framework for cross-modal representation alignment between CT imaging and longitudinal EHR data, designed to generalize across tasks and institutions. CT and EHR modalities are encoded independently using domain-specific foundation models and aligned in a shared latent space through four principled fusion strategies: late fusion, contrastive alignment, cross-attention, and co-attention. We evaluate two clinically distinct TTE tasks: pulmonary embolism (PE) mortality and cardiovascular disease (CVD) outcomes, on large-scale multi-institutional cohorts (PE: N=3,099 train; 1,098 internal; 435 external; CVD: N=2,951 train; 837 internal; 682 external). Fusion consistently improves concordance index by 1.5-5.4% over unimodal baselines when modalities contribute comparably. Overall, contrastive multimodal fusion, particularly with CLMBR representations, provided the most consistent and statistically robust improvements, especially for PE mortality prediction. For MACE, cross-attention (one-hot) achieved the highest internal performance and image-guided co-attention achieved the best external performance. We therefore introduce a generalizable foundation model-based cross-modal alignment framework and provide the first systematic analysis of fusion behavior under modality imbalance in TTE prediction. Our results establish task-aware multimodal alignment as a necessary design principle for robust generalization and scalable clinical deployment.

</details>


### [67] [Risk-Aware LLM Agents for Geospatial Data Retrieval: Design and Preliminary Adversarial Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15077)
*Kyle Gao,Joel Cumming,Jonathan Li,Linlin Xu,David A. Clausi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型的框架，通过自然语言查询从云地理空间目录中检索遥感数据，采用三个智能体（Guardrail、General-QA、Recommender-Analyst）协同工作，将用户意图转换为结构化API调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统遥感数据检索依赖复杂API和结构化查询的问题，降低用户使用门槛，实现自然语言驱动的自动化地球观测数据访问。

Method: 构建三智能体架构：Guardrail负责安全和策略执行，General-QA负责意图理解，Recommender-Analyst负责生成符合模式的API调用；通过API模式替换实现跨平台可移植性。

Result: 在对抗性多轮对话实验中，提示级安全指令提升了鲁棒性，但在API操纵场景中仍存在罕见的高影响故障，表明需要拦截级防护机制。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了用户意图与地理空间基础设施之间的可扩展接口，实现了自动化地球观测工作流，但需进一步强化系统级安全防御以平衡安全性、可用性和成本效率。

Abstract: We present an LLM-driven framework for retrieving remote sensing data from cloud-based geospatial catalogues using natural language queries. The system converts user intent into structured API calls, enabling efficient access to satellite imagery and environmental datasets. The architecture integrates three agents: Guardrail for safety and policy enforcement, General-QA for intent interpretation, and Recommender-Analyst for schema-aware API call generation. This coordinated design ensures reliable, semantically aligned interaction with external data services. The modular framework is portable across platforms through API schema substitution and supports applications in environmental monitoring, disaster response, and climate analysis. It establishes a scalable interface between user intent and geospatial infrastructure, enabling streamlined and automated Earth observation workflows. Preliminary experiments under adversarial multi-turn settings show that prompt-level safety instructions improve robustness, although rare high-impact failures persist in API manipulation scenarios and highlight the need for adaptive, system-level defenses that balance safety, usability, and cost efficiency, which motivates the use of our intercept-level Guardrail agent.

</details>


### [68] [Cognitive Debt: AI as Intellectual Leverage and the Dynamics of Systemic Fragility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15078)
*Shuchen Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立了认知债务的形式化理论，将AI视为替代而非补充第一性原理认知时产生的未验证推理义务存量。模型包含认知资本和认知债务两个状态变量，提出了六个命题：理性代理人承担正向认知债务、认知明斯基时刻、危机损失凸性、假修正循环、分散均衡过度采用替代性AI、高认知资本代理人可能最终侵蚀其无辅助认知资本至低于初始低技能代理人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AI替代人类第一性原理认知时产生的系统性风险，揭示认知债务的形成机制及其对个体和社会层面的负面影响。

Method: 通过构建包含认知资本和认知债务两个状态变量的形式化模型，采用乘数生产技术，将认知资本作为决定AI采用回报的抵押品，建立六个命题进行分析。

Result: 理性代理人因成本递延、部分外部化和短期收益掩盖而承担正向认知债务；平静期降低主观风险评估，提高AI替代强度，形成认知明斯基时刻；危机损失与总杠杆率呈凸性；产出目标压力导致假修正循环；分散均衡因系统性风险、认知公共品和军备竞赛外部性而过度采用替代性AI；高认知资本代理人可能最终无辅助认知资本低于初始低技能代理人。

Conclusion: AI作为认知替代品会产生系统性金融-认知风险，需要关注认知公共品供给和宏观审慎政策干预。

Abstract: We develop a formal theory of cognitive debt: the stock of unverified reasoning obligations that accumulates when individuals use AI as a substitute rather than a complement for first-principles cognition. The model features two state variables per agent, cognitive capital and cognitive debt, and a multiplicative production technology in which cognitive capital functions as collateral that determines the return to AI adoption. We establish six propositions. Rational agents incur positive cognitive debt because the costs are deferred, partially external, and masked by short-run productivity gains. Tranquil periods lower subjective risk assessments, raise AI substitution intensity, and compound leverage, generating a cognitive Minsky moment in which subjective risk falls while true systemic fragility rises. Expected crisis losses are convex in aggregate leverage. Post-crisis, output-target pressure can produce a false-correction loop in which agents patch AI failures with more AI. The decentralised equilibrium over-adopts substitutive AI relative to the social optimum because of systemic risk, cognitive public goods, and arms-race externalities. In a two-type heterogeneous-agent economy, high-cognitive-capital agents adopt AI more intensively and may eventually erode their unaided cognitive capital below that of initially lower-skilled agents.

</details>


### [69] [VGPT-RSI for RH-Adjacent Formal Progress: Boundary Certificates, Verified Finite Lagarias Inequalities, and Explicit Failure Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15096)
*Zhixin Hu,Tao Xu,Xiaodian Sun,Li Jin,Momiao Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可验证的AI辅助推理系统VGPT-RSI，用于对黎曼猜想相关的两个任务生成形式化验证的部分进展：构造并验证有限RH边界证书，以及启动形式化Lagarias路径证书。系统明确识别了剩余未解决的数学障碍，包括形式化Lagarias等价性、证明全局尾部定理等，并强调不作过度声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼猜想是数学核心未解问题，本文不声称解决，而是探索能否通过可验证的AI辅助推理系统产生可靠、经形式化检查的部分进展，同时明确标识剩余数学障碍。

Method: 采用可验证增长物理变换器与递归自改进（VGPT-RSI）框架，应用于两个RH邻接的认证任务：(1) 构造有限RH边界证书，通过向外舍入区间算术和Arb/FLINT球算术审计，并在Rocq/CoqInterval中检验参数化定理；(2) 启动形式化Lagarias路径证书，形式化有限量并在Coq中生成有限证书。

Result: 成功为参数化安全下曲线区域上的不等式构造并验证了有限RH边界证书；启动了形式化Lagarias路径证书，生成了Coq检验的有限证书；明确了剩余数学瓶颈：形式化Lagarias等价性、证明全局尾部定理、减少反例到高度合数或相关极值整数。

Conclusion: VGPT-RSI能够生成经认证的RH邻接形式化进展，组织证明依赖关系，并在剩余障碍确属数学问题时避免过度声明。

Abstract: The Riemann Hypothesis remains one of the central unsolved problems in mathematics. Rather than claiming proof, we investigate whether a verifiable AI-assisted reasoning system can produce reliable, formally checked partial progress while explicitly identifying the remaining mathematical obstructions. We apply the Verifiable Growing Physical Transformer with Recursive Self-Improvement (VGPT-RSI) to two RH-adjacent certification tasks. First, we construct and verify a finite RH-boundary certificate for inequality on a parameterized safe lower curve over a region. The numerical boundary curve is converted into a certificate-backed lower curve, audited using outward-rounded interval arithmetic and Arb/FLINT ball arithmetic, and then checked in Rocq/CoqInterval for the parameterized theorem. Second, we initiate a formal Lagarias-route certificate. Lagarias criterion states that RH is equivalent to the global inequality. We formalize the finite quantity and produce a Coq-checked finite certificate. The final system identifies the exact unresolved mathematical bottlenecks: formalizing the Lagarias equivalence, proving the global tail theorem beyond any finite cutoff, and potentially reducing counterexamples to colossally abundant or related extremal integers. These results demonstrate that VGPT-RSI can produce certified RH-adjacent formal progress, organize proof dependencies, and avoid overclaiming when the remaining obstruction is genuinely mathematical.

</details>


### [70] [Towards Verifiable Agentic Data Science: Solving Irregular TSQA Via Tool-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15107)
*Sanhorn Chen,Xiaoyang Chen,Boyu Liu,Roy Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IRTS-ToolBench，一个包含1700个问题、覆盖13个领域10种任务类型的不规则时间序列问答基准，用于评估大语言模型和AI代理在不规则时间序列数据上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列数据普遍存在不规则性（异步观测、信息性缺失值、变化采样频率），但现有TSQA基准大多假设规则采样，导致对LLM在不规则条件下性能的理解存在根本性空白。

Method: 构建了包含1700个问题的IRTS-ToolBench基准，覆盖13个领域10种任务类型，提供标准化输入和可复现评估协议。

Result: 提供了一个可独立使用的基准测试，填补了不规则时间序列下LLM性能评估的空白。

Conclusion: IRTS-ToolBench弥合了现实不规则时间序列数据与现有规则采样基准之间的差距，为研究LLM在不规则时间序列分析中的表现提供了标准化评估工具。

Abstract: Time series data in real-world deployments is overwhelmingly irregular. Observations are asynchronous, missing values are informative rather than random, and sampling frequencies vary across sensors and operational windows. However, existing Time Series Question Answering (TSQA) benchmarks mostly assume regularly sampled inputs, leaving a fundamental gap in understanding how large language models (LLMs) and AI agents perform under irregular conditions. To bridge this gap, we introduce IRTS-ToolBench, a benchmark of 1,700 questions spanning 10 task types across 13 domains. IRTS-ToolBench is designed to be used independently by any researcher working on LLM-based irregular time series analysis, providing standardized inputs and a reproducible evaluation protocol. Code can be found in https://github.com/SanhornC/IRTS-ToolBench.

</details>


### [71] [CogGuard: Cognitive and Operational Profiling for Proactive Warning in Edge Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15199)
*Zhi Yao,Weihao Chen,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogGuard提出了一种面向边缘智能服务的主动预警框架，将离线LLM驱动的画像构建与在线SLM驱动的分数预测解耦，通过前缀对齐的KV缓存复用和长度感知的分布式微调策略，在教育和操作两大场景中分别降低48%的画像构建时间和19%的分布式微调时间，预测MAE分别达到13.4和5.9（百分制）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方案存在两个问题：(1) 画像构建方法通常领域特定，缺乏跨服务场景的可复用抽象；(2) 在异构边缘集群上微调对齐模型时，因输入序列长度差异导致高同步开销。因此需要一种即满足低延迟和隐私约束，又可复用且高效的边缘预警框架。

Method: CogGuard通过共享的静态-动态画像到分数的流水线（profile-to-score pipeline），将离线LLM画像构建与在线SLM分数预测解耦。采用前缀对齐的KV缓存复用技术减少重复编码开销，并设计长度感知的分布式微调策略结合对比正则化，缓解异构集群上的负载不均衡。

Result: 在教育和操作数据集上，CogGuard将画像构建时间降低48%，分布式微调时间降低19%，在百分制预警任务上MAE分别为13.4和5.9。在最大教育场景中，相比最强基线，预测误差降低15.4%。

Conclusion: CogGuard有效解决了边缘智能服务中主动预警的效率问题，通过LLM与SLM的协同设计与异构集群适配策略，在保证精度的同时大幅降低计算和通信开销，适用于资源受限的边缘部署场景。

Abstract: Proactive warning is an important capability for edge intelligent services, where the system predicts whether a subject will successfully complete an incoming task under strict latency and privacy constraints. Such prediction depends on both long-term static attributes and short-term dynamic states derived from historical interaction logs. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong long-context reasoning for constructing structured profiles from these logs, but existing solutions face two challenges for edge deployment: (1) profiling methods are typically domain-specific and lack a reusable abstraction across service scenarios, and (2) fine-tuning alignment models on heterogeneous edge clusters incurs high synchronization overhead due to the variance in input sequence lengths. To address these challenges, we propose CogGuard, a proactive-warning framework for edge intelligent services. CogGuard decouples offline LLM-based profile construction from online Small Language Model (SLM)-based score prediction through a shared static-dynamic profile-to-score pipeline, and instantiates it in two representative scenarios: educational performance warning and operational task outcome warning. For efficient profile construction, we design scenario-specific profiling methods with prefix-aligned KV-cache reuse to reduce repeated encoding overhead. For edge-side model alignment, we propose a length-aware distributed fine-tuning strategy with contrastive regularization to mitigate workload imbalance on heterogeneous clusters. Experiments on education and operation datasets show that CogGuard reduces profile construction time by up to 48% and distributed fine-tuning time by 19%, while achieving MAEs of 13.4 and 5.9, respectively, on 100-point-scale warning tasks. In the largest educational setting, CogGuard reduces prediction error by 15.4% compared with the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [72] [Mask-Proof: An LLM-based Automated Data Curation Pipeline on Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15258)
*Jierui Zhang,Siyuan Tan,Xinhang Li,Longzhuangzhi Lin,Dailin Li,Chengfeng Gu,Xinping Li,Yaxian Hao,Shengjia Liang,Yuxiang Ren,Wenhao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者提出Mask-Proof，一种将真实数学证明转化为可自动检查的掩码步骤任务的流水线，并构建了包含292个问题的Mask-ProofBench基准。通过17个模型的实验，推理增强模型比标准模型提升12%-27%，评估器与专家标注者的一致率达96.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数学评估多关注最终答案或依赖昂贵的人工评分，缺乏可扩展、可复现的步骤级推理测量方法，难以验证LLM在长证明中的逐步推理能力。

Method: Mask-Proof流水线：从真实证明中掩码关键公式步骤，提供上下文，并使用基于LLM的等价性判断器（通过重复投票保证稳定性）来评估模型的重建结果。

Result: 17个模型在Mask-ProofBench上的实验显示，推理增强模型比标准模型性能提升12%-27%；所提出的LLM评估器与专家标注者一致率达96.8%。

Conclusion: Mask-Proof能够忠实、可复现且可比较地测量步骤级数学推理能力，为可信赖的AI辅助证明验证提供了有效评估工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of mathematical problem solving and can even assist with research-level proofs, yet we still lack a scalable and reproducible way to measure step-level reasoning in long proofs across diverse sources. This evaluation gap limits trustworthy AI assistance in proof-certified scientific progress. Existing evaluations often emphasize final answers or rely on costly expert grading, while end-to-end proof generation remains open-ended and hard to verify automatically. We introduce Mask-Proof, a pipeline that turns real proofs into automatically checkable masked-step tasks. It masks key formula steps, provides the necessary surrounding context, and evaluates model reconstructions with an LLM-based equivalence judge using repeated votes for stability. The resulting Mask-ProofBench contains 292 curated problems across diverse research areas. Experiments with 17 models show that reasoning-enhanced models outperform standard models by 12% to 27%. Our evaluator achieves 96.8% agreement with expert annotators, enabling faithful, reproducible, and comparable measurement of step-level mathematical reasoning. Benchmark, annotations, and code are available at https://github.com/weating/Mask-Proof.

</details>


### [73] [Forced Deferral: Manipulating Routing Decisions in Multimodal LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15308)
*Zhongye Liu,Yaopei Zeng,Yurui Chang,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出强制延迟攻击（FDA），通过对抗性图像触发器降低弱模型置信度，迫使多模态大模型级联系统将查询路由到强模型，从而操纵计算资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型级联系统通过弱模型处理简单查询、强模型处理困难查询来节省计算成本，但弱模型的置信度直接控制计算分配，这暴露了新的攻击面：攻击者可操纵置信度使查询被持续路由到强模型，造成计算资源滥用。

Method: FDA学习一个通用边界触发器，通过优化温度平滑目标函数，将弱模型在触发输入上的token分布推向基于其干净响应构建的、更不集中的目标分布，从而降低弱模型置信度，使级联系统将查询路由到强模型。

Result: 在多个数据集、模型族和延迟指标上，FDA一致地提高了强模型路由率，且优于图像扰动和提示注入基线方法。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型级联系统容易受到操纵计算分配的对抗性攻击，强制非预期的强模型使用，且无需直接影响答案正确性。这揭示了级联系统在安全方面的重要漏洞。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, serving a large model for every query is computationally expensive. MLLM cascades mitigate this cost by first querying a weak but cheaper model and deferring to a strong model when the weak model's output is unconfident. However, since the weak model's confidence directly controls compute allocation, these systems expose a new attack surface: an adversary can manipulate confidence so that their queries are consistently deferred to the strong model. Motivated by this vulnerability, we introduce the Forced Deferral Attack (FDA), an adversarial image attack that lowers the weak model's confidence and causes cascades to route queries to the strong model. FDA learns a universal border trigger by optimizing a temperature-flattened objective. This objective pushes the weak model's token distribution on triggered inputs toward less concentrated targets constructed from its clean responses. Across datasets, model families, and deferral metrics, FDA consistently increases strong-model routing while outperforming image-perturbation and prompt-injection baselines. These results show that MLLM cascades are vulnerable to attacks that manipulate compute allocation, forcing unintended strong-model usage without directly targeting answer correctness.

</details>


### [74] [ChatPlanner: A Large Language Model Framework for Personalized Public Transit Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15315)
*Tingting Yang,Chenhao Xue,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChatPlanner框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）结合检索增强生成（RAG）技术，从自然语言查询中提取用户偏好并将其集成到公共交通路径规划的目标函数中，实现个性化路径推荐。实验表明该框架能可靠生成可行方案，相比现有方法能发现更有价值的多维路线选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化公共交通路径规划面临两大挑战：一是难以捕捉和整合用户多样化偏好（如换乘次数、步行距离、时间偏好等）；二是传统路径规划算法无法理解自然语言中蕴含的细微偏好信息。现有方法缺乏将自然语言理解与路径优化有效结合的范式。

Method: ChatPlanner框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用微调的LLM结合RAG从自然语言查询中提取路径规划参数并解释用户偏好；(2) 将提取的偏好信息集成到路径规划算法的目标函数中；(3) 设计了包含8种人物画像和5种上下文的偏好感知数据集，用于微调和RAG的评分标准建立。通过三个实验分别验证方案可行性、路由信息与偏好提取准确性、以及解集质量和完整性。

Result: (1) ChatPlanner能可靠生成可行路径方案；(2) 微调保证了输出结构的规范性并学习了通用偏好模式；(3) RAG提供了查询特定的上下文信息，能解决模糊或会话式表达问题，并校准连续评分；(4) 微调与RAG相结合在路由信息提取和用户偏好解释方面取得了最高准确率；(5) 相比现有路径规划器，ChatPlanner能识别出不同维度下更有价值的路径选择。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种将自然语言理解融入交通优化的新范式，证明LLM与RAG的组合能够有效弥合用户自然语言表达与路径规划算法之间的语义鸿沟，为个性化公共交通路径规划提供了可行且高效的技术路径。

Abstract: Personalized public transit routing in public transit systems remains challenging due to the difficulty of capturing and integrating diverse user preferences into routing algorithms. This paper presents ChatPlanner, a novel framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to enable preference aware public transit routing. Our approach employs fine-tuned LLMs with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to extract routing parameters and interpret nuanced user preferences from natural language queries, subsequently integrating these preferences into the objective function of a public transit routing algorithm. This study designs preference aware datasets incorporating eight personas and five contexts to establish scoring standards for both fine-tuning and RAG. This work conducted three experiments to validate the solutions' feasibility, extraction of routing information and preferences, and solution set quality and completeness. Results demonstrate that ChatPlanner generates feasible solutions reliably. Fine-tuning enforces the required output structure and learns general preference patterns, while RAG provides query-specific context to resolve imprecise or conversational expressions and calibrate continuous scores. The combination of both achieves the highest accuracy in routing information extraction and user preference interpretation. Results based on selected case studies show that by capturing user preferences, ChatPlanner identifies valuable solutions across different dimensions that existing route planners overlook, generating more valuable route alternatives. This research establishes a new paradigm for integrating natural language understanding into transportation optimization.

</details>


### [75] [APEX: Adaptive Principle EXtraction A Three-Layer Self-Evolution Framework for Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15363)
*Ya-Chuan Chen,Tien-Jen Lai,Hsiang-Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEX是一个三层协同进化框架，同时优化提示词、行为原则和工作流拓扑结构，在Joe AI代理系统上实现APEX健康评分0.570（较基线提升90%），仅需4次LLM调用（约270秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Self-Harness框架仅优化提示词单一维度，未能同步演化行为原则和工作流拓扑，限制了AI代理自我改进的上限。

Method: 提出APEX三层协同进化框架：L1通过失败模式修复优化提示词；L2通过成功轨迹蒸馏提取行为原则；L3通过结构适应度选择进化工作流拓扑。在基于NVIDIA Nemotron的Joe代理系统上，使用114个真实任务轨迹进行18天实验。

Result: APEX健康评分0.570（+90%），蒸馏出6个可复用原则，工作流拓扑评分0.900（+20%），仅需4次LLM调用（~270秒）在本地qwen2.5-coder:32b上运行。

Conclusion: 多维度协同进化显著优于单轴提示词优化，验证了同时进化提示词、原则和工作流拓扑的有效性，且计算成本极低。

Abstract: Self-improvement in AI agents has emerged as a key research frontier: systems that modify their own prompts, workflows, and decision rules based on accumulated operational experience. The state-of-the-art Self-Harness framework [1] achieves 14--21% improvement on Terminal-Bench-2.0 by mining failure clusters and patching the agent harness. However, Self-Harness optimises only one dimension -- the prompt harness -- leaving behavioural principles and workflow topology unchanged. We propose APEX (Adaptive Principle EXtraction), a three-layer co-evolution framework that simultaneously evolves: (L1) the harness via failure-mode patching, (L2) behavioural principles via success-trace distillation [2], and (L3) the agent workflow topology via structural fitness-based selection [6]. We implement APEX on Joe [13], a production-grade super AI Agent built on NVIDIA Nemotron and designed as an Edge AI Agent Factory for the NVIDIA Agent Challenge 2026, managing a 15-node compute fleet using 114 real task traces collected over 18 days. APEX achieves an APEX Health Score of 0.570 (+90% vs. baseline 0.300) in a single evolutionary run, distilling 6 novel reusable principles and selecting a research-first workflow topology scoring 0.900 (+20%). Our results demonstrate that multi-dimensional co-evolution substantially outperforms single-axis harness optimisation, at a cost of only 4 LLM calls (~270 s) on a local qwen2.5-coder:32b instance.

</details>


### [76] [Who Drifted: the System or the Judge? Anytime-Valid Attribution in LLM Evaluation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15474)
*Yitao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解决LLM评估中因裁判模型（judge）版本更新或提示变化导致的指标漂移问题的方法，通过固定人工标注锚点集、双赌博过程（e-process）和守卫窗口规则，实现系统性能退化与裁判变化归因的分离，并验证了其有效性、理论保障和低成本运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM产品的持续评估依赖一个强LLM裁判作为地面真值，但裁判本身也是模型（通过API调用），其版本升级或评分提示的静默更新会导致评分方式变化，使得警报无法区分是产品变差了还是裁判变了，产生歧义。现有方法无法解决这一归因混淆问题。

Method: 1) 固定人工标注锚点集（anchor set），由当前裁判以稳定间隔重新评分；2) 第二个赌博过程（betting e-process）监控裁判与人工评分之间的差距；3) 守卫窗口规则（guard-window rule）输出{无漂移, 系统漂移, 裁判漂移}三种判定。理论上证明了任意有效性、单向识别（只有裁判能移动锚点）、归因竞赛的设计原则（锚点必须跑赢主进程）以及过程正交性。

Result: 在两次真实裁判变化上：静默版本升级在60/60次运行中检测为裁判漂移，零误归因；严格提示变化在守卫窗口宽度300时120次运行中110次正确归因；行业默认的滚动z检验在无漂移流上75%误报。在TL;DR摘要领域完全复现（无需调参），严格提示变化下归因达到完美（240/240）。运行成本约为强裁判评估每项的0.64倍，或在更廉价但灵敏度更低的模式下为0.21倍。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了LLM评估中裁判模型变化与系统性能退化之间的归因混淆问题，具有严格的理论保证（anytime-validity）、低误报率和低成本优势，且在不同领域间具有良好的迁移性，归因竞赛的设计原则在跨域差异中得到了验证。

Abstract: Continuous evaluation of LLM products relies on a strong LLM judge treated as ground truth: a cheap monitor scores every interaction and a team is paged when the score drifts down. But the judge is itself a model behind an API, and a silent version bump or scoring-prompt update changes how it scores -- so every drift alarm is ambiguous between a worse product and a changed judge. We resolve the ambiguity with a fixed, human-labeled anchor set that the current judge re-scores at a steady interleave, a second betting e-process on the judge-versus-human gap, and a guard-window rule returning a verdict in {none, system, judge}. We prove anytime-validity, one-way identification (only the judge can move the anchors), an attribution race whose design law is that the anchors must out-run the main process they guard, and process orthogonality. On two real judge changes, a silent version bump is detected as judge drift in 60/60 runs with zero judge-to-system misattribution, and a contaminating strict-prompt change is correctly attributed on 110 of 120 runs at guard width 300 -- while the industry-default rolling z-test false-alarms on 75% of drift-free streams. Every experiment replicates on a second domain (TL;DR summarization) with nothing re-tuned, and where the domains differ the differences are the ones the race predicts: the strict-prompt change shifts scores harder there, so the anchors fire faster and attribution becomes perfect (240/240). The monitor runs at approximately 0.64 of the cost of strong-judging every item, or 0.21 in a cheaper-but-deafer regime.

</details>


### [77] [Frame-Conditioned Moral Computation in LLaMA 3.1-8B-Instruct: A Mechanistic Interpretability Audit of Ethical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15507)
*Ali Dasdan,Manan Shah,W. Russell Neuman,Chad Coleman,Kund Meghani,Safinah Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过机械可解释性平台Transluce分析LLaMA 3.1-8B-Instruct在54个道德提示上的表现，发现情景锚定效应：领域特定表征主导激活列表顶部，模型的道德标签能力基本恒定，但其显著性高度依赖于提示选择的解释框架。通过多温度审计和跨模型行为代理，提出框架条件性道德计算概念，并呼吁从行为对齐扩展到机械对齐研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的道德提示行为审计仅测量模型的输出内容，而非产生这些输出的内部计算过程。作者旨在探究LLM在道德推理时内部神经表征的运作机制，区分"模型说了什么"与"模型内部如何计算"之间的差异。

Method: 使用Transluce平台对LLaMA 3.1-8B-Instruct进行机械可解释性分析，设计四组道德提示实验（B1:17个困境/政策/元伦理问题；B3:6个角色扮演场景；B4:15个切换机制控制的电车对比；B5:16个身份属性控制的电车对比）。采用两类互补指标：5个聚类级指标和6个神经元级指标面板。还进行了多温度审计和跨模型（两个前沿模型）行为代理分析。

Result: 发现情景锚定效应：领域特定表征在激活列表顶部占主导地位；模型道德标签能力恒定，但显著性受提示框架高度影响。B4与B5对比证实模型关注变化的表面特征，整体道德指标无差异但主导的非道德干扰项反映设计差异。识别出候选道德神经元L16/N3837，跨模型行为代理显示自我报告道德焦点存在差异，与RLHF重排表面文本而不移除底层领域优先框架的一致性对齐包装器假设一致。

Conclusion: 提出框架条件性道德计算：提示的表面词汇选择特征流形，道德结论是这种选择的后续结果。行为对齐必须补充机械对齐——一项研究计划，探究在受控框架变化下，道德相关特征是否可以被证明具有因果优先性，而不仅仅是在解释中表现突出。

Abstract: Behavioral audits of Large Language Models on moral prompts measure what the model says, not the internal computation producing it. We use Transluce, an AI-driven mechanistic-interpretability platform, to examine LLaMA 3.1-8B-Instruct on 54 moral prompts in four batteries: 17 dilemmas, policy, and meta-ethical questions (B1); 6 role-playing scenarios (B3); and a controlled trolley contrast varying the switching mechanism with people fixed (B4, 15 prompts) or identity attributes with mechanism fixed (B5, 16 prompts).
  Two complementary metric families, five cluster-level metrics and a six-metric neuron-level panel, converge on a Situational Anchor Effect: domain-specific representations dominate the top of the activation list across every battery. The model's ethics-labeled capacity stays essentially constant; its salience (rank, priority, top-of-list presence) is highly sensitive to the interpretive frame the prompt selects.
  The B4-vs-B5 contrast confirms the model attends to whichever surface feature varies: aggregate ethics metrics are indistinguishable, but the dominant non-ethics distractor mirrors the design. A multi-temperature audit identifies a candidate ethics neuron (L16/N3837) stable across temperatures; a cross-model behavioral proxy on two frontier models yields preliminary evidence of divergence in self-reported moral focus, consistent with an Alignment Wrapper in which RLHF re-orders surface text without removing underlying domain-first frames. We unify these as Frame-Conditioned Moral Computation: the prompt's surface vocabulary selects a feature manifold, and the moral conclusion is downstream of that selection. Behavioral alignment must be supplemented by Mechanistic Alignment: a research program asking whether ethics-related features can be shown causally privileged under controlled frame variation, not merely loud in the explanation.

</details>


### [78] [ToolMenuBench: Benchmarking Tool-Menu Filtering Strategies for Reliable and Efficient LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15508)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolMenuBench是一个评估多步LLM代理中工具菜单构建的基准，研究工具可见性如何影响可靠性、效率和风险暴露。实验表明，因果最小工具过滤（CMTF）将任务成功率从32.1%提升至85.7%，同时减少约98%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多聚焦于模型能否正确调用工具，忽视了可见工具菜单的构建对代理可靠性、效率和安全风险暴露的影响。本文旨在系统研究工具菜单构建问题。

Method: 构建ToolMenuBench基准，变化工具菜单大小、干扰类型、状态依赖任务结构和风险暴露，在7个模型后端、3种菜单大小、6种过滤方法和7种评估设置下进行受控实验，对比CMTF、词法过滤、状态感知过滤等多种工具过滤方法。

Result: CMTF将任务成功率从所有工具暴露下的32.1%提升至85.7%，平均token使用量减少约98%；因果最小工具过滤在减少可见工具、错误调用、过早动作和风险工具暴露方面取得了最优权衡。

Conclusion: ToolMenuBench提供了一个可复用的评估框架，用于研究代理-接口问题：哪些工具应可见、何时可见以及在何种成本或风险约束下可见。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model agents increasingly operate over large tool libraries, but existing evaluations often focus on whether a model can call a tool correctly rather than how the visible tool menu shapes reliability, efficiency, and safety-relevant risk exposure. We introduce ToolMenuBench, a benchmark for evaluating tool-menu construction in multi-step LLM agents. ToolMenuBench varies tool-menu size, distractor type, state-dependent task structure, and risk exposure, and reports both filter-level and downstream agent metrics, including visible-tool count, risky-tool exposure, task success, wrong-tool calls, premature actions, and token usage. In a controlled evaluation across seven model backends, three tool-menu sizes, six filtering methods, and seven evaluation settings, CMTF improves task success from 32.1% under all-tools exposure to 85.7%, while reducing average token usage by roughly 98%. Causal minimal tool filtering achieves the strongest overall tradeoff, reducing visible tools, wrong-tool calls, premature actions, and risky-tool exposure relative to unfiltered exposure, lexical filtering, state-aware filtering, and broader causal-path baselines. ToolMenuBench provides a reusable evaluation framework for studying the agent-interface problem: which tools should be visible, when they should be visible, and under what cost or risk constraints.

</details>


### [79] [Large Language Models as Optimizers: A Survey of Direct vs. Tool-Augmented Approaches and Their Performance Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15577)
*Roko Peran,Luka Hobor,Mihael Kovac,Mario Brcic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型作为优化器的三种范式：直接优化、工具增强优化和工具创造优化，分析了各自的性能边界与推理能力差距，并讨论了未来模型在效率与可审计性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地参与复杂数学优化问题，而用户可能并未意识到这一点，有必要系统梳理LLM作为优化器的不同范式，识别当前性能边界和推理能力缺陷，为未来发展提供方向性指导。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，将现有LLM优化方法归纳为三种范式：直接优化（迭代提示和启发式生成）、工具增强优化（将自然语言问题转化为形式化规范并调用外部求解器）、工具创造优化（LLM自主发现可复用的算法或启发式策略）。基于已有基准测试描述当前性能前沿。

Result: 识别出当前架构中存在的关键推理能力差距；指出直接优化有未来潜力但可审计性差，工具增强优化可审计性好但灵活性受限；工具创造优化可在零边际LLM成本下部署复用算法。

Conclusion: 未来更强的模型可能倾向于采用工具创造范式，以提高重复性问题的操作效率；需要在直接优化的潜力与工具增强优化的可审计性之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly involved in complex mathematical optimization, even if the pragmatic user who triggers them is unaware of it. After all, many real-world problems reduce to the search for better or the best solutions. The field of LLM-as-optimizer has three paradigms: direct optimization, tool-augmented optimization, and tool-creating optimization. Direct optimization uses iterative prompting and heuristic generation to navigate solution spaces. Tool-augmented optimization translates natural language problems into formal specifications and orchestrates external solvers. Tool-creating optimization goes further, using LLMs to discover reusable algorithms or heuristics that can be deployed at zero marginal LLM cost. We describe current performance frontiers based on the benchmarks from the literature. We identify the critical reasoning gap in current architectures and argue for trade-offs between the future potential of direct optimization and the auditability of tool-augmented optimization. Even future, more powerful models might opt for tool-making to improve operational efficiency for repetitive families of problems.

</details>


### [80] [Your Agent Has a Genome: Sequence-Level Behavioral Analysis and Runtime Governance of LLM-Powered Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15579)
*Sidi Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 XEPV 编码框架（X=探索，E=执行，P=规划，V=验证），将LLM智能体的运行时行为建模为类似基因序列的符号序列。通过对347条生产环境ReAct轨迹的分析，发现三字母组P-X-P是唯一显著的高风险模式（成功率降低10.4%），P比率是最强的负向预测因子，且E→V转移概率仅2.1%表明系统性验证缺失。基于此设计了Governor三层运行时干预系统，实现成功率提升6.2%且token消耗降低44%，并在SWE-bench的2000条轨迹上验证了跨系统泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的行为分析缺乏统一、简洁且可量化的编码框架，难以系统性地识别失败模式和设计干预策略。作者希望借鉴基因组序列分析方法论，将智能体的运行时行为转化为可计算的符号序列，从而发现统计显著的风险模式并指导系统优化。

Method: (1) 提出XEPV四字母编码体系，将智能体行为映射为符号序列；(2) 采用n-gram模式挖掘、马尔可夫转移矩阵、点二列相关系数进行统计分析；(3) 基于发现设计Governor三层干预系统（规则引擎、统计累加器、卡方阈值自适应器）；(4) 在347条生产轨迹和2000条SWE-bench轨迹上进行验证。

Result: (1) P-X-P是唯一统计显著的高风险三字母组，成功率降低10.4%；(2) P-ratio与成功率的负相关性最强（r=-0.256, p<0.0001）；(3) E→V转移概率仅2.1%，存在系统性验证缺失；(4) Governor干预后成功率绝对提升6.2%，token消耗降低44%。

Conclusion: XEPV编码框架能有效捕获LLM智能体的行为模式并识别风险特征，Governor干预系统在提升性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，且该方法具有良好的跨系统泛化性。作者提出了六个未来研究方向，包括碱基序列语言模型、跨智能体行为指纹识别和奖励塑形等。

Abstract: We propose Base Sequence Analysis, a framework that encodes the runtime behavior of LLM-powered autonomous agents into compact symbolic sequences using a four-letter alphabet: X (Explore), E (Execute), P (Plan), and V (Verify). Drawing an analogy to genomic sequence analysis, we apply n-gram pattern mining, Markov transition matrices, and point-biserial correlation to 347 real-world execution traces collected from a production ReAct agent system over 8 days. Our analysis reveals that (1) the trigram P-X-P is the only statistically significant high-risk pattern, lowering success rate by 10.4%; (2) P-ratio is the strongest negative predictor of success (r=-0.256, p<0.0001); and (3) the E->V transition probability is only 2.1%, indicating a systemic verification deficit. Based on these findings, we design Governor, a three-layer runtime intervention system comprising a rule engine, a statistical accumulator, and a chi-square-based threshold adaptor. In a natural before/after deployment evaluation (N=101 vs. N=246), Governor achieves a +6.2% absolute increase in task success rate while simultaneously reducing average token consumption by 44%. To validate cross-system generality, we apply the XEPV encoding to 2,000 public SWE-agent trajectories on SWE-bench, confirming that exploration spirals and the E->V verification deficit replicate in an independent system. We outline six research directions including base sequence language models, cross-agent behavioral fingerprinting, and reward shaping, and release an open-source toolkit for reproducibility.

</details>


### [81] [Agentic Retrieval and Reinforcement Learned Equation Chains: A Controlled Generation Framework for Complex and Novel Physics Word Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15591)
*Tirthankar Mittra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARVRE框架，通过两阶段流程生成高质量物理应用题：第一阶段利用离线时序差分学习构建有效的物理方程链，并结合智能体检索增强生成（RAG）动态选取主题概念；第二阶段由大语言模型将方程链和概念转化为自然语言问题。该方法在保持数学正确性的同时提升了问题的多样性、复杂性和可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成高质量物理应用题（PWP）——兼具新颖性、复杂性和可解性——在教育内容生成领域仍是一个具有挑战性且未被充分探索的问题。现有方法多改编自数学应用题生成，常产生歧义、不可解或结构简单、语言多样性有限的问题。

Method: 两阶段框架ARVRE：第一阶段使用离线时序差分学习构建有效的物理方程链，同时通过智能体检索增强生成（RAG）框架动态选择主题相关概念和词汇；第二阶段由大语言模型将方程链和检索到的概念转化为自然语言物理问题。

Result: 人工评估和自动评估表明，ARVRE生成的物理应用题比现有方法更复杂、更新颖、更可解。

Conclusion: 将强化学习、检索增强生成和大语言模型相结合，有望实现教育物理内容的可靠生成。

Abstract: Generating high-quality Physics Word Problems (PWPs) that are novel, complex, and solvable remains a challenging and underexplored problem in educational content generation. Existing approaches, many adapted from Math Word Problem (MWP) generation, often produce ambiguous, unsolvable, or structurally simple questions with limited linguistic diversity. We introduce ARVRE (Agentic Retrieval Value Reinforced Equation-chain), a two-stage framework for generating diverse and mathematically valid PWPs. In the first stage, a form of offline temporal-difference learning is used to construct valid chains of physics equations, while an agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework dynamically selects topic-specific concepts and vocabulary. This design enables explicit control over problem structure and difficulty. In the second stage, a Large Language Model (LLM) converts the equation chain and retrieved concepts into a natural-language physics question. By grounding generation in valid equation chains, our method preserves mathematical correctness while promoting linguistic diversity and contextual richness. Human and automated evaluations demonstrate that ARVRE generates PWPs that are more complex, novel, and solvable than those produced by existing approaches. These results highlight the potential of combining reinforcement learning, retrieval, and LLMs for reliable generation of educational physics content.

</details>


### [82] [Integrating Reasoning and Generalization in Text-to-SQL via Self-Enhanced Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15598)
*Feng Lyu,Jinfeng Cen,Sijing Duan,Hao Wu,Shucheng Li,Weixu Zhang,Haolun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoTE-SQL，通过自增强推理轨迹、结构化链式思考提示和基于SQL执行反馈的错误修正三大创新，提升了基于LLM的Text-to-SQL性能，在Spider和Bird基准上取得了开源LLM中的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的Text-to-SQL方法难以在强推理能力和稳健泛化能力之间取得平衡，需要一种既能提升推理能力又能保证泛化性的方法。

Method: CoTE-SQL包含三个核心创新：(i) 无需人工标注的、从LLM中蒸馏的自增强推理轨迹；(ii) 结合模块化分解和示例检索的结构化思维链(CoT)提示；(iii) 基于SQL执行反馈的错误感知修正机制。

Result: 在Bird基准上达到53.39% EX / 59.02 VES，在Spider基准上达到79.60% EX / 77.19 VES，均达到开源LLM中同等规模模型的最优性能，尤其在复杂查询上提升显著。

Conclusion: 自增强、结构化推理和执行时反馈三者的结合在基于LLM的Text-to-SQL框架中能够有效提升性能，验证了该设计方向的有效性。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL aims to translate natural language questions into executable SQL queries over structured databases, enabling non-expert users to access data intuitively. While recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in this task, existing LLM-based approaches often struggle to strike a balance between strong reasoning capabilities and robust generalization. To address these limitations, we propose CoTE-SQL to enhance the LLM-based text-to-SQL generation with three key innovations: (i) self-enhanced reasoning traces distilled from LLMs without human annotation, (ii) structured chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting with modular decomposition and examples retrieval, and (iii) error-aware revision based on SQL execution feedback. Extensive experiments on the Spider and Bird benchmarks demonstrate that CoTE-SQL achieves new state-of-the-art performance among methods built on open-source LLMs with comparable model sizes on Bird (53.39% EX / 59.02 VES) and strong results on Spider (79.60% EX / 77.19 VES), with especially significant gains on complex queries. Results highlight the effectiveness of combining self-enhancement, structured reasoning, and execution-time feedback within an LLM-based framework for text-to-SQL design.

</details>


### [83] [NeuroSymbolic AI for Legal AI-TRISM: Trustworthy, Reliable, Interpretable, Safe Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15646)
*Deepa Tilwani,Yash Saxena,Ankur Padia,Srinivasan Parthasarathy,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRISM框架，通过神经符号AI与RAG技术结合，解决大语言模型在法律领域缺乏可解释推理和准确引用验证的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在法律文本分析中缺乏可解释推理且易产生幻觉，一个错误的法律先例引用可能危及案件判决，而现有方法存在结构化法律知识整合不足和生成内容验证机制不充分两大缺陷。

Method: 提出TRISM框架，融合神经符号AI原理与LLM，通过从法律文档中提取符号知识，并结合RASOR RAG生成可解释的推理依据，构建符号化法律知识库，支持可解释推理和输出验证。

Result: 提出了四个贡献：AI在法律领域局限性的分析；RASOR RAG方法（为神经符号RAG生成可形式化为符号表示的可解释推理依据）；符号法律知识库的形式化方法；以及整合符号法律知识与LLM的TRISM框架。

Conclusion: TRISM框架通过神经符号AI与RAG的结合，有望在保持可解释决策路径的同时，提升LLM在法律应用中的可信度、可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed natural language processing, but their lack of interpretable reasoning and tendency to hallucinate pose significant challenges for legal applications. While LLMs show promise for legal text analysis and generation, they struggle with accurate citation attribution and precedent verification. For example, in legal contexts, a single incorrect precedent can jeopardize a case. Current approaches to improve LLM reliability in legal domains suffer from two key limitations: inadequate integration of structured legal knowledge during training or fine-tuning, and insufficient verification mechanisms for generated legal content. To address these challenges, we propose the TRISM (Trustworthy, Reliable, Interpretable, Safe Models) framework, which integrates NeuroSymbolic AI principles with LLMs to leverage both neural learning capabilities and symbolic reasoning over structured legal knowledge. The TRISM approach addresses the above limitations while maintaining interpretable decision pathways. Our framework formalizes the extraction of symbolic knowledge from legal textual documents and incorporates Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) as a core component for grounding LLM outputs in verified legal sources. In this position paper, we make the following contributions: (1) An analysis of the limitations of AI in law; (2) Introduce RASOR RAG which creates foundations for neurosymbolic RAG by generating explicit interpretable rationales that could be formalized into symbolic representations; (3) A formalized methodology for creating symbolic legal knowledge bases that support both interpretable reasoning and output verification in LLMs; and (4) The TRISM framework for integrating symbolic legal knowledge with LLMs.

</details>


### [84] [Towards Next-Generation Healthcare: A Survey of Medical Embodied AI for Perception, Decision-Making, and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15647)
*Cheng Zhang,Qing Cai,Xingzheng Wu,Xun Yang,Xiaojun Chang,Bingkun Bao,Liqiang Nie,Xinwang Liu,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了医学具身人工智能（Medical Embodied AI）领域，聚焦于智能体在临床环境中感知、决策与行动三大核心组件的协调整合，并总结了代表性应用、数据集及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基础模型缺乏与物理世界交互的能力，限制了其在安全关键的真实临床工作流中的应用；而现有的医学具身AI综述多侧重于单一功能组件，缺乏统一的系统级视角来理解智能体如何作为端到端系统在临床环境中运作。

Method: 系统性地综述了医学具身AI的核心组件（感知、决策、行动）及其协调整合方式，并回顾了代表性医疗应用和相关数据集。

Result: 总结出医学具身AI在感知、决策与行动三大组件上的最新进展及其集成框架，整理出代表性临床应用场景和可用数据集，并识别出当前在真实临床实践中面临的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 医学具身AI作为物理交互范式在智能医疗中具有巨大潜力，未来研究需进一步解决组件集成、安全决策等关键问题，以推动其在临床中的实际部署与应用。

Abstract: Foundation models have demonstrated impressive performance in enhancing healthcare efficiency across a wide range of medical applications. Nevertheless, their limited ability to perceive, understand, and interact with the physical world significantly constrains their effectiveness in real-world clinical workflows, where safety-critical decision-making and physical execution are tightly coupled. Recently, embodied artificial intelligence (AI) has emerged as a promising physical-interactive paradigm for intelligent healthcare, enabling agents to operate in complex medical environments. As research in this area rapidly expands, understanding how intelligent agents function as integrated, end-to-end systems in clinical environments becomes increasingly critical. However, existing surveys on medical embodied AI largely emphasize individual aspects or functional components, lacking a unified system-level organization of the field. To support and consolidate recent advances, we systematically survey the core components of medical embodied AI, with a particular emphasis on the coordinated integration of perception, decision-making, and action. We further review representative medical applications and relevant datasets, and we analyze the major challenges encountered in real-world clinical practice. Finally, we discuss key directions for future research in this rapidly evolving field. The associated project can be found at https://github.com/VMVLab/Medical_Embodied_AI_Paper_List.

</details>


### [85] [Multi-agent Framework for Time-Sensitive Complementary Collaboration in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15684)
*Juheon Yi,Jinglu Wang,Xiaoyi Zhang,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TickingCollabBench，一个基于Minecraft的多智能体基准测试，用于评估时间敏感型互补协作任务。该基准反映了真实世界协作的四个核心特征：智能体异质性、强制协作、动态环境以及严格实时约束下的失败风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作基准缺乏对时间敏感互补协作任务的系统性评估，特别是在动态环境、严格实时约束和智能体异质性等真实世界协作特征方面存在空白。

Method: 开发了TickingCollab框架，支持生成多样化动态环境并抽象Minecraft原始API以实现YAML任务声明式配置；设计了可行性感知的自动基准生成管道，由LLM起草多样化任务配置，可行性验证器使用近似约束过滤无效配置。

Result: 实验表明，语言模型延迟以及部分可观测性和智能体异质性下的协调固有难度导致LLM在动态环境中频繁失败，性能显著低于具有全局信息的oracle基线。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在时间敏感互补协作任务上表现不足，亟需改进多智能体协调策略以应对动态环境和实时约束挑战。

Abstract: We present TickingCollabBench, a Minecraft-based multi-agent benchmark for a novel class of time-sensitive complementary collaboration tasks. Our benchmark reflects four core characteristics of real-world collaboration: agent heterogeneity, mandatory collaboration, dynamic environments, and strict real-time constraints with failure risks. To enable this, we develop the TickingCollab framework, which supports the generation of diverse dynamic environments and abstracts Minecraft's primitive APIs to enable declarative YAML task specifications for composing these events. Building on this, we design a feasibility-aware automated benchmark generation pipeline, where an LLM drafts structurally diverse task configurations and feasibility verifier filters out invalid ones using approximate constraints. Evaluations demonstrate that lang latency and inherent difficulty of coordinating under partial observability and agent heterogeneity cause LLMs to frequently fail under dynamic environments and fall significantly short of a global-knowledge oracle.

</details>


### [86] [Do LLMs Reliably Identify Correct Information Units in Aphasic Discourse?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15696)
*Jason M Pittman,Yesenia Medina-Santos,Anton Phillips,Brielle C. Stark*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了指令微调的大语言模型（LLMs）在零样本和少样本提示条件下对失语症语篇进行Correct Information Units（CIU）分类的能力。少样本提示下，Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B的F1分数在0.776-0.817之间，性能接近但尚未达到完全自主可用的水平，可作为人机协同评估系统的组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIU是失语症语篇评估中的核心指标，能够量化交际信息性而非仅关注语言形式，但人工评分耗时且需要经过训练的评价者。本研究旨在探索使用指令微调LLMs自动进行CIU分类的可行性，以降低评分成本和时间。

Method: 使用Cat Rescue刺激材料收集16个图片描述语篇，按四种严重程度（正常、轻度、中度、重度失语症）分层，由人工标注CIU状态。评估四种公开指令微调LLMs（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Phi-3-mini），在零样本和两种少样本提示条件下，使用五个分层随机种子进行测试，以准确率、精确率、召回率、F1和Cohen's kappa评估与人类标注的一致性。

Result: 零样本提示效果不足；少样本提示下三种模型表现良好（平均F1：0.776-0.817），固定全局与分块局部示例选择无显著差异。Phi-3-mini不稳定。可行模型召回率高但精确率较低，存在系统性过度分类为CIU的倾向。严重失语症样本表现最弱。

Conclusion: 少样本LLM提示可在无需梯度训练的情况下支持自动化CIU识别，但与人类标注的一致性尚不足以实现完全自主使用。LLM辅助CIU评分可作为包含人机协同的语篇评估系统的有前景的组件。

Abstract: Correct Information Units (CIUs) are central to discourse assessment in aphasia because they quantify communicative informativeness rather than linguistic form alone. However, CIU scoring is time intensive and requires trained raters. This study examined whether instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) can reliably perform token-level CIU classification from aphasic discourse transcripts. Sixteen picture-description transcripts elicited with the Cat Rescue stimulus were annotated for CIU status according to Nicholas and Brookshire (1993). The sample spanned four severity strata: control, mild, moderate, and severe aphasia. Four publicly available instruction-tuned LLMs were benchmarked under zero-shot and two few-shot prompting conditions across five stratified random seeds. Performance was evaluated against consensus human labels using accuracy, precision, recall, F1, and Cohen's kappa. Zero-shot prompting was insufficient across models. In contrast, few-shot prompting yielded substantial gains and produced competitive performance for three viable models. Mean few-shot F1 scores ranged from 0.776 to 0.817 across Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, and Mistral-7B, with no significant differences between fixed global and per-chunk local example selection. Phi-3-mini was unstable and did not yield reliable performance. Viable models showed high recall but lower precision, suggesting systematic over-classification of tokens as CIUs. Performance also varied by discourse severity, with the weakest results in more severe aphasia. Few-shot LLM prompting can support automated CIU identification without gradient-based task training, but agreement with human annotation remains insufficient for fully autonomous use. These findings support LLM-based CIU scoring as a promising human-in-the-loop component of discourse assessment systems.

</details>


### [87] [AI-Driven Framework for Adaptive Water Network Management with Proof-of-Concept Implementation: Addressing Non-Revenue Water in Jordan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15709)
*Mohammed Fasha,Nahel Al-Maayta,Bilal Sowan,Mohammad Athamneh,Husam Barham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成EPANET水力建模、数字孪生、SCADA系统和基于LLM的AI智能体框架，用于约旦安曼地区非收益水（NRW）的智能监控与决策，在1,164节点配水管网上验证了技术可行性，实现了2分钟内完成异常检测与AI健康报告生成，且零API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约旦面临严重水荒，50%的水因泄漏、盗水和计量问题（非收益水，NRW）而流失，传统被动式方法无法持续有效地降低NRW，亟需智能化解决方案。

Method: 提出智能框架，集成EPANET水力建模、数字孪生技术、SCADA系统和基于LLM的AI智能体；结合实时数据流与物理仿真进行异常检测；采用检索增强生成（RAG）进行策略解读，通过函数调用实现网络控制；在1,164节点安曼区域配水管网上使用EPYT和离线LLM（llama3.1:8b via Ollama）进行概念验证。

Result: 系统实现了自动化水力仿真、基于流量的异常检测（与配水区DZ实践对齐）以及AI生成健康报告，响应时间低于2分钟，零API成本。爆管检测中，30.1 L/s模拟泄漏引起15条管道流量重新分布，标记15节点簇定位爆管位置，验证了与DZ监测实践的一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架适应约旦间歇性供水模式和有限的自动化水平，通过分阶段实施，为水资源匮乏地区提供了一条可扩展的智能化路径，以实现NRW降低和运营效率提升。
